




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

dvaimātikāpāḷi

bhikkhupātimaukkhapāḷi

pubbakaraṇaṃ-4

sammaj‍janī  padīpau ca, udakaṃ āsanaina ca|

upausathassa aitāni, ‘‘pubbakaraṇa’’nti vuc‍cati||

pubbakic‍caṃ-5

chanda, pārisuddhi, utukkhānaṃ, bhikkhugaṇanā ca auvādau|

upausathassa aitāni, ‘‘pubbakic‍ca’’nti vuc‍cati||

pattakal‍laaṅgā-4

upausathau, yāvatikā ca bhikkhū kammappattā|

sabhāgāpattiyau ca na vij‍janti|

vaj‍janīyā ca puggalā tasmiṃ na haunti, ‘‘pattakal‍la’’nti vuc‍cati||

pubbakaraṇapubbakic‍cāni samāpaitvā daisitāpattikassa samaggassa bhikkhusaṅghassa anumatiyā pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ ārādhanaṃ karauma|

nidānuddaisau

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau? aj‍jupausathau pan‍narasau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyya, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyya|

kiṃ saṅghassa pubbakic‍caṃ? pārisuddhiṃ āyasmantau āraucaitha, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmi, taṃ sabbaiva santā sādhukaṃ suṇauma manasi karauma| yassa siyā āpatti, sau āvikaraiyya, asantiyā āpattiyā tuṇhī bhavitabbaṃ, tuṇhībhāvaina khau panāyasmantai ‘‘parisuddhā’’ti vaidissāmi| yathā  khau pana pac‍caikapuṭṭhassa vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti, aivamaivaṃ aivarūpāya parisāya yāvatatiyaṃ anusāvitaṃ hauti| yau pana bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ anusāviyamānai saramānau santiṃ āpattiṃ nāvikaraiyya, sampajānamusāvādassa hauti| sampajānamusāvādau khau panāyasmantau antarāyikau dhammau vuttau bhagavatā, tasmā saramānaina bhikkhunā āpan‍naina visuddhāpaikkhaina santī āpatti āvikātabbā, āvikatā hissa phāsu hauti|

uddiṭṭhaṃ khau āyasmantau nidānaṃ| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

nidānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pārājikuddaisau

tatrimai cattārau pārājikā dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

maithunadhamma sikkhāpadaṃ

1. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau sikkhaṃ appac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivaiyya, antamasau tiracchānagatāyapi, pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau|

adin‍nādānasikkhāpadaṃ

2. yau pana bhikkhu gāmā vā arañ‍ñā vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyya, yathārūpai adin‍nādānai rājānau cauraṃ gahaitvā hanaiyyuṃ vā bandhaiyyuṃ vā pabbājaiyyuṃ vā caurausi bālausi mūḷhausi thainausīti, tathārūpaṃ bhikkhu adin‍naṃ ādiyamānau ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau|

manussaviggahasikkhāpadaṃ

3. yau pana bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, satthahārakaṃ vāssa pariyaisaiyya, maraṇavaṇṇaṃ vā saṃvaṇṇaiyya, maraṇāya vā samādapaiyya ‘‘ambhau purisa kiṃ tuyhiminā pāpakaina duj‍jīvitaina, mataṃ  tai jīvitā saiyyau’’ti, iti cittamanau cittasaṅkappau anaikapariyāyaina maraṇavaṇṇaṃ vā saṃvaṇṇaiyya, maraṇāya vā samādapaiyya, ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau|

uttarimanussadhammasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yau pana bhikkhu anabhijānaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ attupanāyikaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanaṃ samudācaraiyya ‘‘iti jānāmi, iti passāmī’’ti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānau vā asamanuggāhīyamānau vā āpan‍nau visuddhāpaikkhau aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘ajānamaivaṃ āvusau avacaṃ jānāmi, apassaṃ passāmi, tucchaṃ musā vilapi’’nti, añ‍ñatra adhimānā, ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau cattārau pārājikā dhammā| yaisaṃ bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpaj‍jitvā na labhati bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ yathā purai, tathā pacchā, pārājikau  hauti asaṃvāsau| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pārājikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

saṅghādisaisuddaisau

imai khau panāyasmantau tairasa saṅghādisaisā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

suk‍kavissaṭṭhisikkhāpadaṃ

1. sañ‍caitanikā suk‍kavissaṭṭhi añ‍ñatra supinantā saṅghādisaisau|

kāyasaṃsaggasikkhāpadaṃ

2. yau pana bhikkhu autiṇṇau vipariṇataina cittaina mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya hatthaggāhaṃ vā vaiṇiggāhaṃ vā añ‍ñatarassa vā añ‍ñatarassa vā aṅgassa parāmasanaṃ, saṅghādisaisau|

duṭṭhul‍lavācāsikkhāpadaṃ

3. yau  pana bhikkhu autiṇṇau vipariṇataina cittaina mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsaiyya yathā taṃ yuvā yuvatiṃ maithunupasaṃhitāhi, saṅghādisaisau|

attakāmapāricariyasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yau pana bhikkhu autiṇṇau vipariṇataina cittaina mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyya ‘‘aitadaggaṃ bhagini pāricariyānaṃ yā mādisaṃ sīlavantaṃ kalyāṇadhammaṃ brahmacāriṃ aitaina dhammaina paricaraiyyā’’ti maithunupasaṃhitaina, saṅghādisaisau|

sañ‍carittasikkhāpadaṃ

5. yau  pana bhikkhu sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya itthiyā vā purisamatiṃ purisassa vā itthimatiṃ, jāyattanai vā jārattanai vā, antamasau taṅkhaṇikāyapi, saṅghādisaisau|

kuṭikārasikkhāpadaṃ

6. sañ‍ñācikāya pana bhikkhunā kuṭiṃ kārayamānaina assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ pamāṇikā kāraitabbā, tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ, dīghasau dvādasa vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ sattantarā, bhikkhū abhinaitabbā vatthudaisanāya, taihi bhikkhūhi vatthu daisaitabbaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ| sārambhai cai bhikkhu vatthusmiṃ aparik‍kamanai sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kāraiyya, bhikkhū vā anabhinaiyya vatthudaisanāya, pamāṇaṃ vā atik‍kāmaiyya, saṅghādisaisau|

vihārakārasikkhāpadaṃ

7. mahal‍lakaṃ pana bhikkhunā vihāraṃ kārayamānaina sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ bhikkhū abhinaitabbā vatthudaisanāya, taihi bhikkhūhi vatthu daisaitabbaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ| sārambhai cai bhikkhu vatthusmiṃ aparik‍kamanai mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kāraiyya, bhikkhū vā anabhinaiyya vatthudaisanāya, saṅghādisaisau|

duṭṭhadausasikkhāpadaṃ

8. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ duṭṭhau dausau appatītau amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiyya ‘‘appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyā cāvaiyya’’nti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānau vā asamanuggāhīyamānau vā amūlakañ‍caiva taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti, bhikkhu ca dausaṃ patiṭṭhāti, saṅghādisaisau|

añ‍ñabhāgiyasikkhāpadaṃ

9. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ duṭṭhau dausau appatītau añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍cidaisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiyya ‘‘appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyā cāvaiyya’’nti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānau vā asamanuggāhīyamānau vā añ‍ñabhāgiyañ‍caiva taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti kaucidaisau laisamattau upādin‍nau, bhikkhu ca dausaṃ patiṭṭhāti, saṅghādisaisau|

saṅghabhaidasikkhāpadaṃ

10. yau  pana bhikkhu samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kamaiyya, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha tiṭṭhaiyya, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘māyasmā samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha aṭṭhāsi, samaitāyasmā saṅghaina, samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti, aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbau tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānau taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, saṅghādisaisau|

bhaidānuvattakasikkhāpadaṃ

11. tassaiva khau pana bhikkhussa bhikkhū haunti anuvattakā vaggavādakā aikau vā dvai vā tayau vā, tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘māyasmantau aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ kiñ‍ci avacuttha, dhammavādī caisau bhikkhu, vinayavādī caisau bhikkhu, amhākañ‍caisau bhikkhu chandañ‍ca ruciñ‍ca ādāya vauharati, jānāti, nau bhāsati, amhākampaitaṃ khamatī’’ti, tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā ‘‘māyasmantau aivaṃ avacuttha, na caisau bhikkhu dhammavādī, na caisau bhikkhu vinayavādī, māyasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha, samaitāyasmantānaṃ saṅghaina, samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti, aivañ‍ca tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyyuṃ, tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, saṅghādisaisau|

dubbacasikkhāpadaṃ

12. bhikkhu panaiva dubbacajātikau hauti uddaisapariyāpan‍naisu sikkhāpadaisu bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau attānaṃ avacanīyaṃ karauti ‘‘mā maṃ āyasmantau kiñ‍ci avacuttha kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, ahampāyasmantai na kiñ‍ci vakkhāmi kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, viramathāyasmantau mama vacanāyā’’ti, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘māyasmā attānaṃ avacanīyaṃ akāsi, vacanīyamaivāyasmā attānaṃ karautu, āyasmāpi bhikkhū vadatu sahadhammaina, bhikkhūpi āyasmantaṃ vakkhanti sahadhammaina, aivaṃ saṃvaddhā hi tassa bhagavatau parisā yadidaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñavacanaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñavuṭṭhāpanainā’’ti, aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbau tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānau taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, saṅghādisaisau|

kuladūsakasikkhāpadaṃ

13. bhikkhu panaiva añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati kuladūsakau pāpasamācārau, tassa khau pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, kulāni ca taina duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘āyasmā khau kuladūsakau pāpasamācārau, āyasmatau khau pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, kulāni cāyasmatā duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, pak‍kamatāyasmā imamhā āvāsā, alaṃ tai idha vāsainā’’ti, aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tai bhikkhū aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘chandagāminau ca bhikkhū, dausagāminau ca bhikkhū, mauhagāminau ca bhikkhū, bhayagāminau ca bhikkhū tādisikāya āpattiyā aikac‍caṃ pabbājainti, aikac‍caṃ na pabbājaintī’’ti, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, na ca bhikkhū chandagāminau, na ca bhikkhū dausagāminau, na ca bhikkhū mauhagāminau, na ca bhikkhū bhayagāminau, āyasmā khau kuladūsakau pāpasamācārau, āyasmatau  khau pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, kulāni cāyasmatā duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, pak‍kamatāyasmā imamhā āvāsā, alaṃ tai idha vāsainā’’ti, aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbau tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānau taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, saṅghādisaisau|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau tairasa saṅghādisaisā dhammā nava paṭhamāpattikā, cattārau yāvatatiyakā| yaisaṃ bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpaj‍jitvā yāvatīhaṃ jānaṃ paṭicchādaiti, tāvatīhaṃ taina bhikkhunā akāmā parivatthabbaṃ| parivutthaparivāsaina bhikkhunā uttari chārattaṃ bhikkhumānattāya paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ, ciṇṇamānattau bhikkhu yattha siyā vīsatigaṇau bhikkhusaṅghau, tattha sau bhikkhu abbhaitabbau| aikainapi cai ūnau vīsatigaṇau bhikkhusaṅghau taṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, sau ca bhikkhu anabbhitau, tai ca bhikkhū gārayhā, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

saṅghādisaisau niṭṭhitau|

aniyatuddaisau

imai khau panāyasmantau dvai aniyatā dhammā

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

paṭhamaaniyatasikkhāpadaṃ

1. yau  pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai alaṃkammaniyai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, tamainaṃ saddhaiyyavacasā upāsikā disvā tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñataraina vadaiyya pārājikaina vā saṅghādisaisaina vā pācittiyaina vā, nisaj‍jaṃ bhikkhu paṭijānamānau tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñataraina kāraitabbau pārājikaina vā saṅghādisaisaina vā pācittiyaina  vā, yaina vā sā saddhaiyyavacasā upāsikā vadaiyya, taina sau bhikkhu kāraitabbau, ayaṃ dhammau aniyatau|

dutiyaaniyatasikkhāpadaṃ

2. na haiva khau pana paṭicchan‍naṃ āsanaṃ hauti nālaṃkammaniyaṃ, alañ‍ca khau hauti mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsituṃ, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpai āsanai mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, tamainaṃ saddhaiyyavacasā upāsikā disvā dvin‍naṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñataraina vadaiyya saṅghādisaisaina vā pācittiyaina vā, nisaj‍jaṃ bhikkhu paṭijānamānau dvin‍naṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñataraina kāraitabbau saṅghādisaisaina vā pācittiyaina vā, yaina vā sā saddhaiyyavacasā upāsikā vadaiyya, taina sau bhikkhu kāraitabbau, ayampi dhammau aniyatau|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau dvai aniyatā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

aniyatau niṭṭhitau|

nissaggiyapācittiyā

imai khau panāyasmantau tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

kathinasikkhāpadaṃ

1. niṭṭhitacīvarasmiṃ  bhikkhunā ubbhatasmiṃ kathinai dasāhaparamaṃ atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraitabbaṃ, taṃ atik‍kāmayatau nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

udausitasikkhāpadaṃ

2. niṭṭhitacīvarasmiṃ bhikkhunā ubbhatasmiṃ kathinai aikarattampi cai bhikkhu ticīvaraina vippavasaiyya, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

akālacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

3. niṭṭhitacīvarasmiṃ  bhikkhunā ubbhatasmiṃ kathinai bhikkhunau panaiva akālacīvaraṃ uppaj‍jaiyya, ākaṅkhamānaina bhikkhunā paṭiggahaitabbaṃ, paṭiggahaitvā khippamaiva kāraitabbaṃ, nau cassa pāripūri, māsaparamaṃ taina bhikkhunā taṃ cīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ ūnassa pāripūriyā satiyā pac‍cāsāya| tatau cai uttari nikkhipaiyya satiyāpi pac‍cāsāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

purāṇacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhauvāpaiyya vā rajāpaiyya vā ākauṭāpaiyya vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

cīvarapaṭiggahaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

5. yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthatau cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya añ‍ñatra pārivattakā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñātakaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ

6. yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiyya añ‍ñatra  samayā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, acchin‍nacīvarau vā hauti bhikkhu, naṭṭhacīvarau vā, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

tatuttarisikkhāpadaṃ

7. tañ‍cai añ‍ñātakau gahapati vā gahapatānī vā bahūhi cīvaraihi abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya, santaruttaraparamaṃ taina bhikkhunā tatau cīvaraṃ sāditabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari sādiyaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamaupakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

8. bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissa añ‍ñātakassa gahapatissa vā gahapatāniyā vā cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ upakkhaṭaṃ hauti ‘‘iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti, tatra cai sau bhikkhu pubbai appavāritau upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘sādhu vata maṃ āyasmā iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā acchādaihī’’ti kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyaupakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

9. bhikkhuṃ  panaiva uddissa ubhin‍naṃ añ‍ñātakānaṃ gahapatīnaṃ vā gahapatānīnaṃ vā pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍nāni upakkhaṭāni haunti ‘‘imaihi mayaṃ pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihi pac‍caikacīvarāni caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaraihi acchādaissāmā’’ti, tatra cai sau bhikkhu pubbai appavāritau upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘sādhu vata maṃ āyasmantau imaihi pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihi aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā acchādaitha ubhauva santā aikainā’’ti kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

rājasikkhāpadaṃ

10. bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissa rājā vā rājabhauggau vā brāhmaṇau vā gahapatikau vā dūtaina cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ pahiṇaiyya ‘‘iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaraina acchādaihī’’ti| sau cai dūtau taṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘idaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ, paṭiggaṇhātu āyasmā cīvaracaitāpan‍na’’nti| taina bhikkhunā sau dūtau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ paṭiggaṇhāma, cīvarañ‍ca khau mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma kālaina kappiya’’nti| sau cai dūtau  taṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘atthi panāyasmatau kauci vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti| cīvaratthikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau niddisitabbau ārāmikau vā upāsakau vā ‘‘aisau khau, āvusau, bhikkhūnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti| sau cai dūtau taṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmā vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ niddisi, sañ‍ñattau sau mayā, upasaṅkamatāyasmā kālaina, cīvaraina taṃ acchādaissatī’’ti| cīvaratthikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau upasaṅkamitvā dvattikkhattuṃ caudaitabbau sāraitabbau ‘‘atthau mai, āvusau, cīvarainā’’ti, dvattikkhattuṃ caudayamānau sārayamānau taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai abhinipphādaiyya, catukkhattuṃ pañ‍cakkhattuṃ chakkhattuparamaṃ tuṇhībhūtaina uddissa ṭhātabbaṃ, catukkhattuṃ pañ‍cakkhattuṃ chakkhattuparamaṃ tuṇhībhūtau uddissa tiṭṭhamānau taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, tatau cai uttari vāyamamānau taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| nau  cai abhinipphādaiyya, yatassa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ, tattha sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ, dūtau vā pāhaitabbau ‘‘yaṃ khau tumhai āyasmantau bhikkhuṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ pahiṇittha, na taṃ tassa bhikkhunau kiñ‍ci atthaṃ anubhauti, yuñ‍jantāyasmantau sakaṃ, mā vau sakaṃ vinassā’’ti, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

kathinavaggau paṭhamau|

kausiyasikkhāpadaṃ

11. yau pana bhikkhu kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

suddhakāḷakasikkhāpadaṃ

12. yau pana bhikkhu suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

dvaibhāgasikkhāpadaṃ

13. navaṃ pana bhikkhunā santhataṃ kārayamānaina dvai bhāgā suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ ādātabbā, tatiyaṃ audātānaṃ, catutthaṃ gaucariyānaṃ| anādā cai bhikkhu dvai bhāgai suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ, tatiyaṃ audātānaṃ, catutthaṃ gaucariyānaṃ, navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

chabbassasikkhāpadaṃ

14. navaṃ pana bhikkhunā santhataṃ kārāpaitvā chabbassāni dhāraitabbaṃ, auraina cai chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ  taṃ santhataṃ vissaj‍jaitvā vā avissaj‍jaitvā vā añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nisīdanasanthatasikkhāpadaṃ

15. nisīdanasanthataṃ pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthi ādātabbā dubbaṇṇakaraṇāya| anādā cai bhikkhu purāṇasantha tassa  sāmantā sugatavidatthiṃ, navaṃ nisīdanasanthataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

aiḷakalaumasikkhāpadaṃ

16. bhikkhunau panaiva addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nassa aiḷakalaumāni uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, ākaṅkhamānaina bhikkhunā paṭiggahaitabbāni, paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaparamaṃ sahatthā haritabbāni asantai hārakai| tatau cai uttari haraiyya, asantaipi hārakai, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

aiḷakalaumadhauvāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

17. yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaiyya vā rajāpaiyya vā vijaṭāpaiyya vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

rūpiyasikkhāpadaṃ

18. yau pana bhikkhu jātarūparajataṃ uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā upanikkhittaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

rūpiyasaṃvauhārasikkhāpadaṃ

19. yau pana bhikkhu nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kayavik‍kayasikkhāpadaṃ

20. yau pana bhikkhu nānappakārakaṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kausiyavaggau dutiyau|

pattasikkhāpadaṃ

21. dasāhaparamaṃ  atiraikapattau dhāraitabbau, taṃ atik‍kāmayatau nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ūnapañ‍cabandhanasikkhāpadaṃ

22. yau pana bhikkhu ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| taina bhikkhunā sau pattau bhikkhuparisāya nissaj‍jitabbau, yau ca tassā bhikkhuparisāya pattapariyantau, sau tassa bhikkhunau padātabbau ‘‘ayaṃ tai bhikkhu pattau yāva bhaidanāya dhāraitabbau’’ti, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

bhaisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

23. yāni khau pana tāni gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭisāyanīyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ, tāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaparamaṃ san‍nidhikārakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbāni, taṃ atik‍kāmayatau nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

vassikasāṭikasikkhāpadaṃ

24. ‘‘māsau saisau gimhāna’’nti bhikkhunā vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ, ‘‘addhamāsau saisau gimhāna’’nti katvā nivāsaitabbaṃ| auraina cai ‘‘māsau saisau gimhāna’’nti vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisaiyya, auraina‘‘ddhamāsau saisau gimhāna’’nti katvā nivāsaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

cīvaraacchindanasikkhāpadaṃ

25. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindaiyya vā acchindāpaiyya vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

suttaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ

26. yau pana bhikkhu sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

mahāpaisakārasikkhāpadaṃ

27. bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissa añ‍ñātakau gahapati vā gahapatānī vā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ  vāyāpaiyya, tatra cai sau bhikkhu pubbai appavāritau tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘idaṃ khau, āvusau, cīvaraṃ maṃ uddissa viyyati, āyatañ‍ca karautha, vitthatañ‍ca, appitañ‍ca, suvītañ‍ca, suppavāyitañ‍ca, suvilaikhitañ‍ca, suvitacchitañ‍ca karautha, appaiva nāma mayampi āyasmantānaṃ kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyyāmā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu vatvā kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyya antamasau piṇḍapātamattampi, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ac‍caikacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

28. dasāhānāgataṃ  kattikataimāsikapuṇṇamaṃ bhikkhunau panaiva ac‍caikacīvaraṃ uppaj‍jaiyya, ac‍caikaṃ mañ‍ñamānaina bhikkhunā paṭiggahaitabbaṃ, paṭiggahaitvā yāva cīvarakālasamayaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari nikkhipaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

sāsaṅkasikkhāpadaṃ

29. upavassaṃ khau pana kattikapuṇṇamaṃ yāni khau pana tāni ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanāni sāsaṅkasammatāni sappaṭibhayāni, tathārūpaisu bhikkhu saināsanaisu viharantau ākaṅkhamānau tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipaiyya, siyā ca tassa bhikkhunau kaucidaiva pac‍cayau taina cīvaraina vippavāsāya, chārattaparamaṃ taina bhikkhunā taina cīvaraina vippavasitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari vippavasaiyya añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pariṇatasikkhāpadaṃ

30. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pattavaggau tatiyau|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

nissaggiyapācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

suddhapācittiyā

imai khau panāyasmantau dvainavuti pācittiyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

musāvādasikkhāpadaṃ

1. sampajānamusāvādai  pācittiyaṃ|

aumasavādasikkhāpadaṃ

2. aumasavādai pācittiyaṃ|

paisuñ‍ñasikkhāpadaṃ

3. bhikkhupaisuñ‍ñai  pācittiyaṃ|

padasaudhammasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yau pana bhikkhu anupasampan‍naṃ padasau dhammaṃ vācaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasahasaiyyasikkhāpadaṃ

5. yau pana bhikkhu anupasampan‍naina uttaridirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasahasaiyyasikkhāpadaṃ

6. yau pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dhammadaisanāsikkhāpadaṃ

7. yau pana bhikkhu mātugāmassa uttarichappañ‍cavācāhi dhammaṃ daisaiyya añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñunā purisaviggahaina, pācittiyaṃ|

bhūtāraucanasikkhāpadaṃ

8. yau pana bhikkhu anupasampan‍nassa uttarimanussadhammaṃ āraucaiyya, bhūtasmiṃ pācittiyaṃ|

duṭṭhul‍lāraucanasikkhāpadaṃ

9. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaiyya añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, pācittiyaṃ|

pathavīkhaṇanasikkhāpadaṃ

10. yau pana bhikkhu pathaviṃ khaṇaiyya vā khaṇāpaiyya vā pācittiyaṃ|

musāvādavaggau paṭhamau|

bhūtagāmasikkhāpadaṃ

11. bhūtagāmapātabyatāya pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñavādakasikkhāpadaṃ

12. añ‍ñavādakai, vihaisakai pācittiyaṃ|

ujjhāpanakasikkhāpadaṃ

13. ujjhāpanakai, khiyyanakai pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ

14. yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā bhisiṃ vā kaucchaṃ vā ajjhaukāsai santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya, na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ

15. yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya, na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

anupakhaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

16. yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai jānaṃ pubbupagataṃ bhikkhuṃ anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaiyya  ‘‘yassa sambādhau bhavissati, sau pak‍kamissatī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

nik‍kaḍḍhanasikkhāpadaṃ

17. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ kupitau anattamanau saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhaiyya vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

vaihāsakuṭisikkhāpadaṃ

18. yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

mahal‍lakavihārasikkhāpadaṃ

19. mahal‍lakaṃ pana bhikkhunā vihāraṃ kārayamānaina yāva dvārakausā aggaḷaṭṭhapanāya ālaukasandhiparikammāya dvatticchadanassa pariyāyaṃ appaharitai ṭhitaina adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ, tatau cai uttari appaharitaipi ṭhitau adhiṭṭhahaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ

20. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍caiyya vā siñ‍cāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

bhūtagāmavaggau dutiyau|

auvādasikkhāpadaṃ

21. yau  pana bhikkhu asammatau bhikkhuniyau auvadaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

atthaṅgatasikkhāpadaṃ

22. sammataupi cai bhikkhu atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

bhikkhunupassayasikkhāpadaṃ

23. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadaiyya añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, gilānā hauti bhikkhunī, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

āmisasikkhāpadaṃ

24. yau pana bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantī’’ti, pācittiyaṃ|

cīvaradānasikkhāpadaṃ

25. yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ dadaiyya añ‍ñatra pārivattakā, pācittiyaṃ|

cīvarasibbanasikkhāpadaṃ

26. yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbaiyya vā sibbāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

saṃvidhānasikkhāpadaṃ

27. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya antamasau gāmantarampi añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, satthagamanīyau hauti maggau, sāsaṅkasammatau, sappaṭibhayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

nāvābhiruhanasikkhāpadaṃ

28. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhaiyya uddhaṃgāminiṃ vā adhaugāminiṃ vā añ‍ñatra tiriyaṃ taraṇāya, pācittiyaṃ|

paripācitasikkhāpadaṃ

29. yau  pana bhikkhu jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhā, pācittiyaṃ|

rahaunisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

30. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

auvādavaggau tatiyau|

āvasathapiṇḍasikkhāpadaṃ

31. agilānaina bhikkhunā aikau āvasathapiṇḍau bhuñ‍jitabbau| tatau cai uttari bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

gaṇabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

32. gaṇabhaujanai añ‍ñatra samayā pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, nāvābhiruhanasamayau, mahāsamayau, samaṇabhattasamayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

paramparabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

33. paramparabhaujanai añ‍ñatra samayā pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

kāṇamātusikkhāpadaṃ

34. bhikkhuṃ panaiva kulaṃ upagataṃ pūvaihi vā manthaihi vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya, ākaṅkhamānaina bhikkhunā dvattipattapūrā paṭiggahaitabbā| tatau cai uttari paṭiggaṇhaiyya, pācittiyaṃ| dvattipattapūrai paṭiggahaitvā tatau nīharitvā bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvibhajitabbaṃ, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

paṭhamapavāraṇāsikkhāpadaṃ

35. yau pana bhikkhu bhuttāvī pavāritau anatirittaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyapavāraṇāsikkhāpadaṃ

36. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina khādanīyaina vā bhaujanīyaina vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya ‘‘handa bhikkhu khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti jānaṃ āsādanāpaikkhau, bhuttasmiṃ pācittiyaṃ|

vikālabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

37. yau pana bhikkhu vikālai khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

san‍nidhikārakasikkhāpadaṃ

38. yau pana bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

paṇītabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

39. yāni khau pana tāni paṇītabhaujanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi, navanītaṃ, tailaṃ, madhu, phāṇitaṃ, macchau, maṃsaṃ, khīraṃ, dadhi| yau pana bhikkhu aivarūpāni paṇītabhaujanāni agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dantapaunasikkhāpadaṃ

40. yau pana bhikkhu adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharaiyya añ‍ñatra udakadantapaunā, pācittiyaṃ|

bhaujanavaggau catutthau|

acailakasikkhāpadaṃ

41. yau pana bhikkhu acailakassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā dadaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

uyyaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

42. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti  tassa dāpaitvā vā adāpaitvā vā uyyaujaiyya ‘‘gacchāvusau, na mai tayā saddhiṃ kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hauti, aikakassa mai kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hautī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

sabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

43. yau  pana bhikkhu sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

rahaupaṭicchan‍nasikkhāpadaṃ

44. yau pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

rahaunisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

45. yau pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

cārittasikkhāpadaṃ

46. yau pana bhikkhu nimantitau sabhattau samānau santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

mahānāmasikkhāpadaṃ

47. agilānaina bhikkhunā catumāsappac‍cayapavāraṇā sāditabbā añ‍ñatra punapavāraṇāya, añ‍ñatra nic‍capavāraṇāya| tatau cai uttari sādiyaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

uyyuttasaināsikkhāpadaṃ

48. yau pana bhikkhu uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchaiyya añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, pācittiyaṃ|

saināvāsasikkhāpadaṃ

49. siyā  ca tassa bhikkhunau kaucidaiva pac‍cayau sainaṃ gamanāya, dirattatirattaṃ taina bhikkhunā saināya vasitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari vasaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

uyyaudhikasikkhāpadaṃ

50. dirattatirattaṃ cai bhikkhu saināya vasamānau uyyaudhikaṃ vā balaggaṃ vā sainābyūhaṃ vā anīkadassanaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

acailakavaggau pañ‍camau|

surāpānasikkhāpadaṃ

51. surāmairayapānai  pācittiyaṃ|

aṅgulipataudakasikkhāpadaṃ

52. aṅgulipataudakai pācittiyaṃ|

hasadhammasikkhāpadaṃ

53. udakai hasadhammai pācittiyaṃ|

anādariyasikkhāpadaṃ

54. anādariyai pācittiyaṃ|

bhiṃsāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

55. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

jautisikkhāpadaṃ

56. yau pana bhikkhu agilānau visibbanāpaikkhau jautiṃ samādahaiyya vā samādahāpaiyya vā añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, pācittiyaṃ|

nahānasikkhāpadaṃ

57. yau  pana bhikkhu aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau ‘‘diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhāna’’nti ‘‘vassānassa paṭhamau māsau’’ ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau, pariḷāhasamayau, gilānasamayau, kammasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, vātavuṭṭhisamayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

dubbaṇṇakaraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

58. navaṃ pana bhikkhunā cīvaralābhaina tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ ādātabbaṃ nīlaṃ vā kaddamaṃ vā kāḷasāmaṃ vā| anādā cai bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

vikappanasikkhāpadaṃ

59. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā sikkhamānāya vā sāmaṇairassa vā sāmaṇairiyā vā sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

apanidhānasikkhāpadaṃ

60. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaiyya vā apanidhāpaiyya vā antamasau hasāpaikkhaupi, pācittiyaṃ|

surāpānavaggau chaṭṭhau|

sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ

61. yau pana bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ

62. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

uk‍kauṭanasikkhāpadaṃ

63. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

duṭṭhul‍lasikkhāpadaṃ

64. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

ūnavīsativassasikkhāpadaṃ

65. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaiyya, sau ca puggalau anupasampan‍nau, tai ca bhikkhū gārayhā, idaṃ tasmiṃ pācittiyaṃ|

thaiyyasatthasikkhāpadaṃ

66. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya antamasau gāmantarampi, pācittiyaṃ|

saṃvidhānasikkhāpadaṃ

67. yau pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya antamasau gāmantarampi, pācittiyaṃ|

ariṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

68. yau  pana bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya, anaikapariyāyaināvusau antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbau tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānau taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

ukkhittasambhaugasikkhāpadaṃ

69. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ tathāvādinā bhikkhunā akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jaiyya vā, saṃvasaiyya vā, saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

kaṇṭakasikkhāpadaṃ

70. samaṇuddaisaupi  cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti, sau samaṇuddaisau bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘māvusau, samaṇuddaisa aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya, anaikapariyāyaināvusau, samaṇuddaisa antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti, aivañ‍ca sau samaṇuddaisau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sau samaṇuddaisau bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘aj‍jataggai tai, āvusau, samaṇuddaisa na caiva sau bhagavā satthā apadisitabbau, yampi cañ‍ñai samaṇuddaisā labhanti bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ dirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ, sāpi tai natthi, cara pirai, vinassā’’ti| yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaiyya vā, upaṭṭhāpaiyya vā, sambhuñ‍jaiyya vā, saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sappāṇakavaggau sattamau|

sahadhammikasikkhāpadaṃ

71. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi, yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti, pācittiyaṃ| sikkhamānaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā añ‍ñātabbaṃ paripucchitabbaṃ paripañhitabbaṃ, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

vilaikhanasikkhāpadaṃ

72. yau pana bhikkhu pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘kiṃ panimaihi khuddānukhuddakaihi sikkhāpadaihi uddiṭṭhaihi, yāvadaiva kuk‍kuc‍cāya vihaisāya vilaikhāya saṃvattantī’’ti, sikkhāpadavivaṇṇakai pācittiyaṃ|

mauhanasikkhāpadaṃ

73. yau pana bhikkhu anvaddhamāsaṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘idānaiva khau ahaṃ jānāmi, ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’’ti| tañ‍cai bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñai bhikkhū jānaiyyuṃ nisin‍napubbaṃ iminā bhikkhunā dvattikkhattuṃ  pātimaukkhai uddissamānai, kau pana vādau bhiyyau, na ca tassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñāṇakaina mutti atthi, yañ‍ca tattha āpattiṃ āpan‍nau, tañ‍ca yathādhammau kāraitabbau, uttari cassa mauhau āraupaitabbau ‘‘tassa tai, āvusau, alābhā, tassa tai dul‍laddhaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānaina sādhukaṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi karausī’’ti, idaṃ tasmiṃ mauhanakai pācittiyaṃ|

pahārasikkhāpadaṃ

74. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa kupitau anattamanau pahāraṃ dadaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

talasattikasikkhāpadaṃ

75. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa kupitau anattamanau talasattikaṃ uggiraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

amūlakasikkhāpadaṃ

76. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ

77. yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahaiyya ‘‘itissa muhuttampi aphāsu bhavissatī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

upassutisikkhāpadaṃ

78. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhaiyya ‘‘yaṃ imai bhaṇissanti, taṃ saussāmī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

kammappaṭibāhanasikkhāpadaṃ

79. yau pana bhikkhu dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

chandaṃadatvāgamanasikkhāpadaṃ

80. yau  pana bhikkhu saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dubbalasikkhāpadaṃ

81. yau pana bhikkhu samaggaina saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘yathāsanthutaṃ bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇāmaintī’’ti, pācittiyaṃ|

pariṇāmanasikkhāpadaṃ

82. yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sahadhammikavaggau aṭṭhamau|

antaipurasikkhāpadaṃ

83. yau pana bhikkhu rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa anikkhantarājakai aniggataratanakai pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau indakhīlaṃ atik‍kāmaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

ratanasikkhāpadaṃ

84. yau  pana bhikkhu ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā añ‍ñatra ajjhārāmā vā ajjhāvasathā vā uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ| ratanaṃ vā pana bhikkhunā ratanasammataṃ vā ajjhārāmai vā ajjhāvasathai vā uggahaitvā vā uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipitabbaṃ ‘‘yassa bhavissati, sau harissatī’’ti, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

vikālagāmappavaisanasikkhāpadaṃ

85. yau pana bhikkhu santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchāvikālai gāmaṃ pavisaiyya añ‍ñatra tathārūpā ac‍cāyikā karaṇīyā, pācittiyaṃ|

sūcigharasikkhāpadaṃ

86. yau  pana bhikkhu aṭṭhimayaṃ vā dantamayaṃ vā visāṇamayaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ kārāpaiyya, bhaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

mañ‍capīṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

87. navaṃ pana bhikkhunā mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā kārayamānaina aṭṭhaṅgulapādakaṃ kāraitabbaṃ sugataṅgulaina añ‍ñatra haiṭṭhimāya aṭaniyā| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

tūlaunaddhasikkhāpadaṃ

88. yau pana bhikkhu mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaiyya, uddālanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nisīdanasikkhāpadaṃ

89. nisīdanaṃ pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina pamāṇikaṃ kāraitabbaṃ, tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ, dīghasau dvai vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ diyaḍḍhaṃ, dasā vidatthi| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kaṇḍuppaṭicchādisikkhāpadaṃ

90. kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiṃ pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina pamāṇikā kāraitabbā, tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ, dīghasau catassau vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ dvai vidatthiyau| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

vassikasāṭikasikkhāpadaṃ

91. vassikasāṭikaṃ  pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina pamāṇikā kāraitabbā, tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ, dīghasau cha vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ aḍḍhataiyyā| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nandasikkhāpadaṃ

92. yau pana bhikkhu sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ kārāpaiyya, atiraikaṃ vā, chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ| tatridaṃ sugatassa sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ, dīghasau nava vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ cha vidatthiyau, idaṃ sugatassa sugatacīvarappamāṇanti|

ratanavaggau navamau|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau dvainavuti pācittiyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

pāṭidaisanīyā

imai khau panāyasmantau cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

paṭhamapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

1. yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā ‘‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’ti|

dutiyapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

2. bhikkhū panaiva kulaisu nimantitā bhuñ‍janti, tatra cai sā bhikkhunī vausāsamānarūpā ṭhitā hauti ‘‘idha sūpaṃ daitha, idha audanaṃ daithā’’ti| taihi bhikkhūhi sā bhikkhunī apasādaitabbā ‘‘apasak‍ka tāva bhagini, yāva  bhikkhū bhuñ‍jantī’’ti| aikassapi cai bhikkhunau na paṭibhāsaiyya taṃ bhikkhuniṃ apasādaituṃ ‘‘apasak‍ka tāva bhagini, yāva bhikkhū bhuñ‍jantī’’ti, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taihi bhikkhūhi ‘‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jimhā asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimā’’ti|

tatiyapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

3. yāni  khau pana tāni saikkhasammatāni kulāni, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpaisu saikkhasammataisu kulaisu pubbai animantitau agilānau khādanīyaṃ vā, bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā, bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā ‘‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’ti|

catutthapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yāni khau pana tāni ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanāni sāsaṅkasammatāni sappaṭibhayāni, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpaisu saināsanaisu pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā, bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā agilānau khādaiyya vā, bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā ‘‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’ti|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pāṭidaisanīyā niṭṭhitā|

saikhiyā

imai khau panāyasmantau saikhiyā dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

parimaṇḍalasikkhāpadaṃ

1. parimaṇḍalaṃ nivāsaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

2. parimaṇḍalaṃ pārupissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

suppaṭicchan‍nasikkhāpadaṃ

3. suppaṭicchan‍nau  antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

4. suppaṭicchan‍nau antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

susaṃvutasikkhāpadaṃ

5. susaṃvutau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

6. susaṃvutau antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

aukkhittacakkhusikkhāpadaṃ

7. aukkhittacakkhu antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

8. aukkhittacakkhu antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ukkhittakasikkhāpadaṃ

9. na ukkhittakāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

10. na ukkhittakāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

parimaṇḍalavaggau paṭhamau|

uj‍jagghikasikkhāpadaṃ

11. na uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

12. na uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uc‍casaddasikkhāpadaṃ

13. appasaddau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

14. appasaddau  antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

kāyappacālakasikkhāpadaṃ

15. na kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

16. na kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

bāhuppacālakasikkhāpadaṃ

17. na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

18. na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharainisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sīsappacālakasikkhāpadaṃ

19. na  sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

20. na sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uj‍jagghikavaggau dutiyau|

khambhakatasikkhāpadaṃ

21. na khambhakatau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

22. na khambhakatau antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

auguṇṭhitasikkhāpadaṃ

23. na auguṇṭhitau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

24. na auguṇṭhitau antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uk‍kuṭikasikkhāpadaṃ

25. na  uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pal‍latthikasikkhāpadaṃ

26. na pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sak‍kac‍capaṭiggahaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

27. sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pattasañ‍ñīpaṭiggahaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

28. pattasañ‍ñī piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

samasūpakapaṭiggahaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

29. samasūpakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

samatittikasikkhāpadaṃ

30. samatittikaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

khambhakatavaggau tatiyau|

sak‍kac‍cabhuñ‍janasikkhāpadaṃ

31. sak‍kac‍caṃ  piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pattasañ‍ñībhuñ‍janasikkhāpadaṃ

32. pattasañ‍ñī piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sapadānasikkhāpadaṃ

33. sapadānaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

samasūpakasikkhāpadaṃ

34. samasūpakaṃ  piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

nathūpakatasikkhāpadaṃ

35. na thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

audanappaṭicchādanasikkhāpadaṃ

36. na sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaissāmi bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāyāti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sūpaudanaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ

37. na sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ujjhānasañ‍ñīsikkhāpadaṃ

38. na ujjhānasañ‍ñī paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

kabaḷasikkhāpadaṃ

39. nātimahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ālaupasikkhāpadaṃ

40. parimaṇḍalaṃ ālaupaṃ karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sak‍kac‍cavaggau catutthau|

anāhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

41. na  anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivarissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

bhuñ‍jamānasikkhāpadaṃ

sakabaḷasikkhāpadaṃ

piṇḍukkhaipakasikkhāpadaṃ

kabaḷāvacchaidakasikkhāpadaṃ

avagaṇḍakārakasikkhāpadaṃ

hatthaniddhunakasikkhāpadaṃ

jivhānicchārakasikkhāpadaṃ

capucapukārakasikkhāpadaṃ

50. na capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

kabaḷavaggau pañ‍camau|

surusurukārakasikkhāpadaṃ

hatthanil‍laihakasikkhāpadaṃ

pattanil‍laihakasikkhāpadaṃ

53. na  pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

auṭṭhanil‍laihakasikkhāpadaṃ

54. na auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sāmisasikkhāpadaṃ

55. na sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sasitthakasikkhāpadaṃ

56. na sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

chattapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

57. na chattapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

daṇḍapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

58. na daṇḍapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

satthapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

59. na satthapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

āvudhapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

60. na āvudhapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

surusuruvaggau chaṭṭhau|

pādukasikkhāpadaṃ

61. na  pādukāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

upāhanasikkhāpadaṃ

62. na  upāhanāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

yānasikkhāpadaṃ

63. na yānagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sayanasikkhāpadaṃ

64. na sayanagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pal‍latthikasikkhāpadaṃ

65. na pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

vaiṭhitasikkhāpadaṃ

66. na vaiṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

auguṇṭhitasikkhāpadaṃ

67. na auguṇṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

chamāsikkhāpadaṃ

68. na chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

nīcāsanasikkhāpadaṃ

69. na nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ṭhitasikkhāpadaṃ

70. na ṭhitau nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pacchataugamanasikkhāpadaṃ

71. na  pacchatau gacchantau puratau gacchantassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uppathainagamanasikkhāpadaṃ

72. na uppathaina gacchantau pathaina gacchantassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ṭhitauuc‍cārasikkhāpadaṃ

73. na ṭhitau agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

haritaiuc‍cārasikkhāpadaṃ

74. na haritai agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

udakaiuc‍cārasikkhāpadaṃ

75. na udakai agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pādukavaggau sattamau|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau saikhiyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

saikhiyā niṭṭhitā|

adhikaraṇasamathā

imai khau panāyasmantau satta adhikaraṇasamathā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ  adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāya sammukhāvinayau dātabbau|

sativinayau dātabbau|

amūḷhavinayau dātabbau|

paṭiñ‍ñāya  kāraitabbaṃ|

yaibhuyyasikā|

tassapāpiyasikā|

tiṇavatthārakauti|

uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai, pucchāmi kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

adhikaraṇasamathā niṭṭhitā|

uddiṭṭhaṃ khau āyasmantau nidānaṃ,

uddiṭṭhā cattārau pārājikā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā tairasa saṅghādisaisā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā dvai aniyatā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā dvainavuti pācittiyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā saikhiyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā, aittakaṃ tassa bhagavatau suttāgataṃ suttapariyāpan‍naṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ  uddaisaṃ āgacchati, tattha sabbaihaiva samaggaihi sammaudamānaihi avivadamānaihi sikkhitabbanti|

vitthāruddaisau pañ‍camau|

bhikkhupātimaukkhaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

bhikkhunīpātimaukkhapāḷi

pubbakaraṇaṃ-4

sammaj‍janī  padīpau ca, udakaṃ āsanaina ca|

upausathassa aitāni, ‘‘pubbakaraṇa’’nti vuc‍cati||

pubbakic‍caṃ-5

chanda, pārisuddhi, utukkhānaṃ, bhikkhunigaṇanā ca auvādau|

upausathassa aitāni, ‘‘pubbakic‍ca’’nti vuc‍cati||

pattakal‍laaṅgā-4

upausathau, yāvatikā ca bhikkhunī kammappattā|

sabhāgāpattiyau ca na vij‍janti|

vaj‍janīyā ca puggalā tasmiṃ na haunti, ‘‘pattakal‍la’’nti vuc‍cati||

pubbakaraṇapubbakic‍cāni samāpaitvā daisitāpattikassa samaggassa bhikkhunisaṅghassa anumatiyā pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ ārādhanaṃ karauma|

nidānuddaisau

suṇātu  mai ayyai saṅghau, aj‍jupausathau pan‍narasau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyya, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyya|

kiṃ saṅghassa pubbakic‍caṃ? pārisuddhiṃ ayyāyau āraucaitha, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmi, taṃ sabbāva santā sādhukaṃ suṇauma manasi karauma| yassā siyā āpatti, sā āvikaraiyya, asantiyā āpattiyā tuṇhī bhavitabbaṃ, tuṇhībhāvaina khau panāyyāyau, ‘‘parisuddhā’’ti vaidissāmi| yathā khau pana pac‍caikapuṭṭhassā vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti, aivamaivaṃ aivarūpāya parisāya yāvatatiyaṃ anusāvitaṃ hauti| yā pana bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ anusāviyamānai saramānā santiṃ āpattiṃ nāvikaraiyya, sampajānamusāvādassā hauti| sampajānamusāvādau khau panāyyāyau, antarāyikau dhammau  vuttau bhagavatā, tasmā saramānāya bhikkhuniyā āpan‍nāya visuddhāpaikkhāya santī āpatti āvikātabbā, āvikatā hissā phāsu hauti|

uddiṭṭhaṃ khau, ayyāyau, nidānaṃ| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

nidānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pārājikuddaisau

tatrimai aṭṭha pārājikā dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

maithunadhammasikkhāpadaṃ

1. yā pana bhikkhunī chandasau maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivaiyya, antamasau tiracchānagatainapi, pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā|

adin‍nādānasikkhāpadaṃ

2. yā  pana bhikkhunī gāmā vā arañ‍ñā vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyya, yathārūpai adin‍nādānai rājānau cauraṃ gahaitvā hanaiyyuṃ vā bandhaiyyuṃ vā pabbājaiyyuṃ vā caurāsi bālāsi mūḷhāsi thaināsīti, tathārūpaṃ bhikkhunī adin‍naṃ ādiyamānā ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā|

manussaviggahasikkhāpadaṃ

3. yā pana bhikkhunī sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, satthahārakaṃ vāssa pariyaisaiyya, maraṇavaṇṇaṃ vā saṃvaṇṇaiyya, maraṇāya vā samādapaiyya ‘‘ambhau purisa, kiṃ tuyhiminā pāpakaina duj‍jīvitaina, mataṃ tai jīvitā saiyyau’’ti, iti cittamanā cittasaṅkappā anaikapariyāyaina maraṇavaṇṇaṃ vā saṃvaṇṇaiyya, maraṇāya vā samādapaiyya, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā|

uttarimanussadhammasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yā pana bhikkhunī anabhijānaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ attupanāyikaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanaṃ samudācaraiyya ‘‘iti jānāmi, iti passāmī’’ti, tatau  aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānā vā asamanuggāhīyamānā vā āpan‍nā visuddhāpaikkhā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘ajānamaivaṃ, ayyai, avacaṃ jānāmi, apassaṃ passāmi, tucchaṃ musā vilapi’’nti, añ‍ñatra adhimānā, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā|

ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalikāsikkhāpadaṃ

5. yā pana bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa, adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ āmasanaṃ vā parāmasanaṃ vā gahaṇaṃ vā chupanaṃ vā paṭipīḷanaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalikā|

vaj‍jappaṭicchādikāsikkhāpadaṃ

6. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaiyya, na gaṇassa āraucaiyya, yadā ca sā ṭhitā vā assa cutā vā nāsitā vā avassaṭā vā, sā pacchā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘pubbaivāhaṃ, ayyai, añ‍ñāsiṃ aitaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ‘aivarūpā ca  aivarūpā ca sā bhaginī’ti, nau ca khau attanā paṭicaudaissaṃ, na gaṇassa āraucaissa’’nti, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā|

ukkhittānuvattikāsikkhāpadaṃ

7. yā pana bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādaraṃ appaṭikāraṃ akatasahāyaṃ tamanuvattaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘aisau khau, ayyai, bhikkhu samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittau, dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādarau appaṭikārau akatasahāyau, māyyai, aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ anuvattī’’ti, aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyaṃ cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā ukkhittānuvattikā|

aṭṭhavatthukāsikkhāpadaṃ

8. yā  pana bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthaggahaṇaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, saṅghāṭikaṇṇaggahaṇaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, santiṭṭhaiyya vā, sal‍lapaiyya vā, saṅkaitaṃ vā gacchaiyya, purisassa vā abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyaiyya, chan‍naṃ vā anupavisaiyya, kāyaṃ vā tadatthāya upasaṃharaiyya aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā aṭṭhavatthukā|

uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, aṭṭha pārājikā dhammā| yaisaṃ bhikkhunī añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpaj‍jitvā na labhati bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ yathā purai, tathā pacchā, pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā| tatthāyyāyau, pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pārājikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

saṅghādisaisuddaisau

imai khau panāyyāyau sattarasa saṅghādisaisā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

ussayavādikāsikkhāpadaṃ

1. yā  pana bhikkhunī ussayavādikā viharaiyya gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā dāsaina vā kammakāraina vā antamasau samaṇaparibbājakaināpi, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

caurīvuṭṭhāpikāsikkhāpadaṃ

2. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ cauriṃ vajjhaṃ viditaṃ anapalaukaitvā rājānaṃ vā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā pūgaṃ vā saiṇiṃ vā, añ‍ñatra kappā vuṭṭhāpaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

aikagāmantaragamanasikkhāpadaṃ

3. yā  pana bhikkhunī aikā vā gāmantaraṃ gacchaiyya, aikā vā nadīpāraṃ gacchaiyya, aikā vā rattiṃ vippavasaiyya, aikā vā gaṇamhā auhiyaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

ukkhittakaausāraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yā pana bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ, anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

bhaujanapaṭiggahaṇapaṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

5. yā pana bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā, bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

bhaujanapaṭiggahaṇadutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

6. yā  pana bhikkhunī aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā, iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā, taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

sañ‍carittasikkhāpadaṃ

7. yā pana bhikkhunī sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya itthiyā vā purisamatiṃ, purisassa vā itthimatiṃ, jāyattanai vā jārattanai vā antamasau taṅkhaṇikāyapi, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

duṭṭhadausasikkhāpadaṃ

8. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniṃ duṭṭhā dausā appatītā amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiyya ‘‘appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyā cāvaiyya’’nti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānā vā asa manuggāhīyamānā vā amūlakañ‍caiva taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti, bhikkhunī ca dausaṃ  patiṭṭhāti, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

añ‍ñabhāgiyasikkhāpadaṃ

9. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniṃ duṭṭhā dausā appatītā añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍cidaisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiyya ‘‘appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyā cāvaiyya’’nti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānā vā asamanuggāhīyamānā vā añ‍ñabhāgiyañ‍caiva taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti| kaucidaisau laisamattau upādin‍nau, bhikkhunī ca dausaṃ patiṭṭhāti, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

sikkhaṃpac‍cācikkhaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

10. yā pana bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau, santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ  santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’’ti| sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyai kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca ‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau, santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’ti, abhiramāyyai, svākkhātau dhammau, cara brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti, aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

adhikaraṇakupitasikkhāpadaṃ

11. yā pana bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyai, kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca ‘chandagāminiyau  ca bhikkhuniyau, dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’ti, ayyā khau chandāpi gacchaiyya, dausāpi gacchaiyya, mauhāpi gacchaiyya, bhayāpi gacchaiyyā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

pāpasamācārapaṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

12. bhikkhuniyau panaiva saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā, tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi aivamassu vacanīyā ‘‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā, vivic‍cathāyyai, vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti, aivañ‍ca tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyyuṃ, tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai  samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, imāpi bhikkhuniyau yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

pāpasamācāradutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

13. yā pana bhikkhunī aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva, ayyai, tumhai viharatha, mā tumhai nānā viharittha, santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā, tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā dubbalyā aivamāha – ‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā, vivic‍cathāyyai, vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’’ti, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyai, aivaṃ avaca, saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai tumhai viharatha, mā tumhai nānā viharittha, santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā, tā  saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha, tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā dubbalyā aivamāha – ‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā, vivic‍cathāyyai, vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’’ti, aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

saṅghabhaidakasikkhāpadaṃ

14. yā pana bhikkhunī samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kamaiyya, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha tiṭṭhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyā, samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha aṭṭhāsi, samaitāyyā, saṅghaina, samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā  taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

bhaidānuvattakasikkhāpadaṃ

15. tassāyaiva khau pana bhikkhuniyā bhikkhuniyau haunti anuvattikā vaggavādikā aikā vā dvai vā tissau vā, tā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘māyyāyau, aitaṃ bhikkhuniṃ kiñ‍ci avacuttha dhammavādinī caisā bhikkhunī, vinayavādinī caisā bhikkhunī, amhākañ‍caisā bhikkhunī chandañ‍ca ruciñ‍ca ādāya vauharati, jānāti, nau bhāsati, amhākampaitaṃ khamatī’’ti, tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi aivamassu vacanīyā ‘‘māyyāyau, aivaṃ avacuttha, na caisā bhikkhunī dhammavādinī, na caisā bhikkhunī vinayavādinī, māyyānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha, samaitāyyānaṃ saṅghaina, samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti, aivañ‍ca tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyyuṃ, tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi  yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ| ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, imāpi bhikkhuniyau yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

dubbacasikkhāpadaṃ

16. bhikkhunī panaiva dubbacajātikā hauti uddaisapariyāpan‍naisu sikkhāpadaisu bhikkhunīhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānā attānaṃ avacanīyaṃ karauti ‘‘mā maṃ ayyāyau kiñ‍ci avacuttha kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, ahampāyyāyau, na kiñ‍ci vakkhāmi kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, viramathāyyāyau, mama vacanāyā’’ti, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyā, attānaṃ avacanīyaṃ akāsi, vacanīyamaiva, ayyā, attānaṃ karautu, ayyāpi bhikkhuniyau vadatu sahadhammaina, bhikkhuniyaupi ayyaṃ vakkhanti sahadhammaina, aivaṃ saṃvaddhā hi tassa bhagavatau parisā yadidaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñavacanaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñavuṭṭhāpanainā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

kuladūsakasikkhāpadaṃ

17. bhikkhunī  panaiva añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati kuladūsikā pāpasamācārā, tassā khau pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, kulāni ca tāya duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘ayyā, khau kuladūsikā pāpasamācārā, ayyāya khau pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, kulāni cāyyāya, duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, pak‍kamatāyyā imamhā āvāsā, alaṃ tai idha vāsainā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tā bhikkhuniyau aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, tādisikāya āpattiyā aikac‍caṃ pabbājainti aikac‍caṃ na pabbājaintī’’ti, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyā, aivaṃ avaca, na ca bhikkhuniyau chandagāminiyau, na ca bhikkhuniyau dausagāminiyau, na ca bhikkhuniyau mauhagāminiyau, na ca bhikkhuniyau bhayagāminiyau, ayyā khau kuladūsikā pāpasamācārā, ayyāya khau pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, kulāni cāyyāya duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca, pak‍kamatāyyā, imamhā āvāsā alaṃ tai idha vāsainā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ|

uddiṭṭhā khau ayyāyau sattarasa saṅghādisaisā dhammā nava paṭhamāpattikā, aṭṭha yāvatatiyakā,

yaisaṃ bhikkhunī añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpaj‍jati, tāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai pakkhamānattaṃ caritabbaṃ| ciṇṇamānattā bhikkhunī yattha siyā vīsatigaṇau bhikkhunisaṅghau, tattha sā bhikkhunī abbhaitabbā| aikāyapi cai ūnau vīsatigaṇau bhikkhunisaṅghau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ abbhaiyya, sā ca bhikkhunī anabbhitā, tā ca bhikkhuniyau gārayhā, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi, pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

saṅghādisaisau niṭṭhitau|

nissaggiya pācittiyā

imai khau panāyyāyau tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

pattasan‍nicayasikkhāpadaṃ

1. yā  pana bhikkhunī pattasan‍nicayaṃ karaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

akālacīvarabhājanasikkhāpadaṃ

2. yā pana bhikkhunī akālacīvaraṃ ‘‘kālacīvara’’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

cīvaraparivattanasikkhāpadaṃ

3. yā  pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ cīvaraṃ parivattaitvā sā pacchā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘handāyyai, tuyhaṃ cīvaraṃ, āhara maitaṃ cīvaraṃ, yaṃ tuyhaṃ tuyhamaivaitaṃ, yaṃ mayhaṃ mayhamaivaitaṃ, āhara maitaṃ cīvaraṃ, sakaṃ pac‍cāharā’’ti acchindaiyya vā acchindāpaiyya vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñaviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yā pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñacaitāpana sikkhāpadaṃ

5. yā pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasaṅghikacaitāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

6. yā pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasaṅghikacaitāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

7. yā  pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamagaṇikacaitāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

8. yā pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyagaṇikacaitāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

9. yā pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

puggalikacaitāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

10. yā pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina puggalikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pattavaggau paṭhamau|

garupāvuraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

11. garupāvuraṇaṃ  pana bhikkhuniyā caitāpaintiyā catuk‍kaṃsaparamaṃ caitāpaitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

lahupāvuraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

12. lahupāvuraṇaṃ pana bhikkhuniyā caitāpaintiyā aḍḍhataiyyakaṃsaparamaṃ caitāpaitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kathinasikkhāpadaṃ

13. niṭṭhitacīvarasmiṃ bhikkhuniyā ubbhatasmiṃ kathinai dasāhaparamaṃ atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

udausitasikkhāpadaṃ

14. niṭṭhitacīvarasmiṃ  bhikkhuniyā ubbhatasmiṃ kathinai aikarattampi cai bhikkhunī ticīvaraina vippavasaiyya, añ‍ñatra bhikkhunisammutiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

akālacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

15. niṭṭhitacīvarasmiṃ bhikkhuniyā ubbhatasmiṃ kathinai bhikkhuniyā panaiva akālacīvaraṃ uppaj‍jaiyya, ākaṅkhamānāya bhikkhuniyā paṭiggahaitabbaṃ, paṭiggahaitvā khippamaiva kāraitabbaṃ, nau cassa pāripūri, māsaparamaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā taṃ cīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ ūnassa pāripūriyā satiyā pac‍cāsāya| tatau cai uttari nikkhipaiyya satiyāpi pac‍cāsāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñātakaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ

16. yā pana bhikkhunī añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiyya añ‍ñatra samayā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau acchin‍nacīvarā vā hauti bhikkhunī, naṭṭhacīvarā vā, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

tatuttarisikkhāpadaṃ

17. tañ‍cai añ‍ñātakau gahapati vā gahapatānī vā bahūhi cīvaraihi abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya, santaruttaraparamaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā tatau cīvaraṃ sāditabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari sādiyaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamaupakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

18. bhikkhuniṃ  panaiva uddissa añ‍ñātakassa gahapatissa vā gahapatāniyā vā cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ upakkhaṭaṃ hauti ‘‘iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti| tatra caisā bhikkhunī pubbai appavāritā upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘sādhu vata, maṃ āyasmā iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā acchādaihī’’ti kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyaupakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

19. bhikkhuniṃ  panaiva uddissa ubhin‍naṃ añ‍ñātakānaṃ gahapatīnaṃ vā gahapatānīnaṃ vā pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍nāni upakkhaṭāni haunti ‘‘imaihi mayaṃ pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihi pac‍caikacīvarāni caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ cīvaraihi acchādaissāmā’’ti| tatra caisā bhikkhūnī pubbai appavāritā upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘sādhu vata maṃ āyasmantau imaihi pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihi aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā acchādaitha ubhauva santā aikainā’’ti kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

rājasikkhāpadaṃ

20. bhikkhuniṃ panaiva uddissa rājā vā rājabhauggau vā brāhmaṇau vā gahapatikau vā dūtaina cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ pahiṇaiyya ‘‘iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ cīvaraina acchādaihī’’ti| sau cai dūtau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘idaṃ khau, ayyai, ayyaṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ, paṭiggaṇhātāyyā cīvaracaitāpan‍na’’nti| tāya bhikkhuniyā sau dūtau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ paṭiggaṇhāma, cīvarañ‍ca khau mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma kālaina kappiya’’nti| sau cai dūtau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘atthi panāyyāya, kauci vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti, cīvaratthikāya, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau niddisitabbau ārāmikau vā upāsakau vā ‘‘aisau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunīnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti| sau cai dūtau taṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘yaṃ khau, ayyai, ayyā vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ niddisi, sañ‍ñattau sau mayā, upasaṅkamatāyyā  kālaina, cīvaraina taṃ acchādaissatī’’ti| cīvaratthikāya, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau upasaṅkamitvā dvattikkhattuṃ caudaitabbau sāraitabbau ‘‘atthau mai, āvusau, cīvarainā’’ti, dvattikkhattuṃ caudayamānā sārayamānā taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai abhinipphādaiyya, catukkhattuṃ pañ‍cakkhattuṃ chakkhattuparamaṃ tuṇhībhūtāya uddissa ṭhātabbaṃ, catukkhattuṃ pañ‍cakkhattuṃ chakkhattuparamaṃ tuṇhībhūtā uddissa tiṭṭhamānā taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| tatau cai uttari vāyamamānā taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| nau cai abhinipphādaiyya, yatassā cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ, tattha sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ, dūtau vā pāhaitabbau ‘‘yaṃ khau tumhai āyasmantau bhikkhuniṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ  pahiṇittha, na taṃ tassā bhikkhuniyā kiñ‍ci atthaṃ anubhauti, yuñ‍jantāyasmantau sakaṃ, mā vau sakaṃ vinassā’’ti, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

cīvaravaggau dutiyau|

rūpiyasikkhāpadaṃ

21. yā pana bhikkhunī jātarūparajataṃ uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā upanikkhittaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

rūpiyasaṃvauhārasikkhāpadaṃ

22. yā pana bhikkhunī nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kayavik‍kayasikkhāpadaṃ

23. yā pana bhikkhunī nānappakārakaṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ūnapañ‍cabandhanasikkhāpadaṃ

24. yā pana bhikkhunī ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| tāya bhikkhuniyā sau pattau bhikkhuniparisāya nissaj‍jitabbau, yau ca tassā bhikkhuniparisāya pattapariyantau, sau tassā bhikkhuniyā padātabbau ‘‘ayaṃ tai bhikkhuni pattau yāvabhaidanāya dhāraitabbau’’ti, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

bhaisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

25. yāni  khau pana tāni gilānānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ paṭisāyanīyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ, tāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaparamaṃ san‍nidhikārakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbāni| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

cīvaraacchindanasikkhāpadaṃ

26. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā acchindaiyya vā acchindāpaiyya vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

suttaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ

27. yā  pana bhikkhunī sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

mahāpaisakārasikkhāpadaṃ

28. bhikkhuniṃ panaiva uddissa añ‍ñātakau gahapati vā gahapatānī vā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaiyya, tatra caisā bhikkhunī pubbai appavāritā tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘idaṃ khau āvusau cīvaraṃ maṃ uddissa viyyati, āyatañ‍ca karautha, vitthatañ‍ca appitañ‍ca suvītañ‍ca suppavāyitañ‍ca suvilaikhitañ‍ca suvitacchitañ‍ca karautha, appaiva nāma mayampi āyasmantānaṃ kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyyāmā’’ti, aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī vatvā kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyya antamasau piṇḍapātamattampi, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ac‍caikacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

29. dasāhānāgataṃ kattikataimāsikapuṇṇamaṃ bhikkhuniyā panaiva ac‍caikacīvaraṃ uppaj‍jaiyya, ac‍caikaṃ mañ‍ñamānāya bhikkhuniyā paṭiggahaitabbaṃ, paṭiggahaitvā yāva cīvarakālasamayaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari nikkhipaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pariṇatasikkhāpadaṃ

30. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pattavaggau tatiyau|

uddiṭṭhā  khau, ayyāyau, tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau, pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

nissaggiyapācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

suddhapācittiyā

imai khau panāyyāyau, chasaṭṭhisatā pācittiyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

lasuṇasikkhāpadaṃ

1. yā  pana bhikkhunī lasuṇaṃ khādaiyya pācittiyaṃ|

sambādhalaumasikkhāpadaṃ

2. yā pana bhikkhunī sambādhai laumaṃ saṃharāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

talaghātakasikkhāpadaṃ

3. talaghātakai pācittiyaṃ|

jatumaṭṭhakasikkhāpadaṃ

4. jatumaṭṭhakai pācittiyaṃ|

udakasuddhikasikkhāpadaṃ

5. udakasuddhikaṃ pana bhikkhuniyā ādiyamānāya dvaṅgulapabbaparamaṃ ādātabbaṃ| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā pācittiyaṃ|

upatiṭṭhanasikkhāpadaṃ

6. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jantassa pānīyaina vā vidhūpanaina vā upatiṭṭhaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

āmakadhañ‍ñasikkhāpadaṃ

7. yā pana bhikkhunī āmakadhañ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñatvā vā viñ‍ñāpaitvā vā bhaj‍jitvā vā bhaj‍jāpaitvā vā kauṭṭaitvā vā kauṭṭāpaitvā vā pacitvā vā pacāpaitvā vā bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamauc‍cārachaḍḍanasikkhāpadaṃ

8. yā  pana bhikkhunī uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā saṅkāraṃ vā vighāsaṃ vā tiraukuṭṭai vā tiraupākārai vā chaḍḍaiyya vā chaḍḍāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyauc‍cārachaḍḍanasikkhāpadaṃ

9. yā pana bhikkhunī uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā saṅkāraṃ vā vighāsaṃ vā haritai chaḍḍaiyya vā chaḍḍāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

nac‍cagītasikkhāpadaṃ

10. yā pana bhikkhunī nac‍caṃ vā gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

lasuṇavaggau paṭhamau|

rattandhakārasikkhāpadaṃ

11. yā pana bhikkhunī rattandhakārai appadīpai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhaiyya vā sal‍lapaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭicchan‍naukāsasikkhāpadaṃ

12. yā pana bhikkhunī paṭicchan‍nai aukāsai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhaiyya vā sal‍lapaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

ajjhaukāsasal‍lapanasikkhāpadaṃ

13. yā pana bhikkhunī ajjhaukāsai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhaiyya vā sal‍lapaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyikauyyaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

14. yā pana bhikkhunī rathikāya vā byūhai vā siṅghāṭakai vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhaiyya vā sal‍lapaiyya vā nikaṇṇikaṃ vā jappaiyya dutiyikaṃ vā bhikkhuniṃ uyyaujaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

anāpucchāpak‍kamanasikkhāpadaṃ

15. yā  pana bhikkhunī puraibhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā āsanai nisīditvā sāmikai anāpucchā pak‍kamaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

anāpucchāabhinisīdanasikkhāpadaṃ

16. yā  pana bhikkhunī pacchābhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā āsanai abhinisīdaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

anāpucchāsantharaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

17. yā pana bhikkhunī vikālai kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā abhinisīdaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

paraujjhāpanakasikkhāpadaṃ

18. yā pana bhikkhunī duggahitaina dūpadhāritaina paraṃ ujjhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paraabhisapanasikkhāpadaṃ

19. yā pana bhikkhunī attānaṃ vā paraṃ vā nirayaina vā brahmacariyaina vā abhisapaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

raudanasikkhāpadaṃ

20. yā pana bhikkhunī attānaṃ vadhitvā vadhitvā raudaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

rattandhakāravaggau dutiyau|

naggasikkhāpadaṃ

21. yā pana bhikkhunī naggā nahāyaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

udakasāṭikasikkhāpadaṃ

22. udakasāṭikaṃ pana bhikkhuniyā kārayamānāya pamāṇikā kāraitabbā, tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ, dīghasau catassau vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ dvai vidatthiyau| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

cīvarasibbanasikkhāpadaṃ

23. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ visibbaitvā vā visibbāpaitvā vā sā pacchā anantarāyikinī naiva sibbaiyya, na sibbāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya añ‍ñatra catūhapañ‍cāhā, pācittiyaṃ|

saṅghāṭicārasikkhāpadaṃ

24. yā  pana bhikkhunī pañ‍cāhikaṃ saṅghāṭicāraṃ atik‍kāmaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

cīvarasaṅkamanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

25. yā pana bhikkhunī cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

gaṇacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

26. yā pana bhikkhunī gaṇassa cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ karaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭibāhanasikkhāpadaṃ

27. yā pana bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

cīvaradānasikkhāpadaṃ

28. yā pana bhikkhunī agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā samaṇacīvaraṃ dadaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

kālaatik‍kamanasikkhāpadaṃ

29. yā pana bhikkhunī dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsāya cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

kathinuddhārasikkhāpadaṃ

30. yā  pana bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

naggavaggau tatiyau|

aikamañ‍catuvaṭṭanasikkhāpadaṃ

31. yā pana bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaiyyuṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

aikattharaṇatuvaṭṭanasikkhāpadaṃ

32. yā pana bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaiyyuṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

aphāsukaraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

33. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

naupaṭṭhāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

34. yā  pana bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhahaiyya, na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

nik‍kaḍḍhanasikkhāpadaṃ

35. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhaiyya vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

saṃsaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ
