36. yā pana bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā viharaiyya gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyai, saṃsaṭṭhā vihari gahapatināpi gahapatiputtaināpi, vivic‍cāyyai, vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginiyā saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya, yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

antauraṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

37. yā  pana bhikkhunī antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ caraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

tirauraṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

38. yā pana bhikkhunī tirauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ caraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

antauvassasikkhāpadaṃ

39. yā pana bhikkhunī antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ caraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

cārikanapak‍kamanasikkhāpadaṃ

40. yā pana bhikkhunī vassaṃvuṭṭhā cārikaṃ na pak‍kamaiyya antamasau chappañ‍cayaujanānipi, pācittiyaṃ|

tuvaṭṭavaggau catutthau|

rājāgārasikkhāpadaṃ

41. yā  pana bhikkhunī rājāgāraṃ vā cittāgāraṃ vā ārāmaṃ vā uyyānaṃ vā paukkharaṇiṃ vā dassanāya gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

āsandiparibhuñ‍janasikkhāpadaṃ

42. yā pana bhikkhunī āsandiṃ vā pal‍laṅkaṃ vā paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

suttakantanasikkhāpadaṃ

43. yā pana bhikkhunī suttaṃ kantaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

gihivaiyyāvac‍casikkhāpadaṃ

44. yā pana bhikkhunī gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

adhikaraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

45. yā  pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā ‘‘aihāyyai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’’ti vuc‍camānā ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā sā pacchā anantarāyikinī naiva vūpasamaiyya, na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

bhaujanadānasikkhāpadaṃ

46. yā pana bhikkhunī agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā dadaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

āvasathacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

47. yā pana bhikkhunī āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jaitvā paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

āvasathavihārasikkhāpadaṃ

48. yā pana bhikkhunī āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

tiracchānavij‍jāpariyāpuṇanasikkhāpadaṃ

49. yā pana bhikkhunī tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

tiracchānavij‍jāvācanasikkhāpadaṃ

50. yā pana bhikkhunī tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

cittāgāravaggau pañ‍camau|

ārāmapavisanasikkhāpadaṃ

51. yā  pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ sabhikkhukaṃ ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

bhikkhuak‍kausanasikkhāpadaṃ

52. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuṃ ak‍kausaiyya vā paribhāsaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

gaṇaparibhāsanasikkhāpadaṃ

53. yā  pana bhikkhunī caṇḍīkatā gaṇaṃ paribhāsaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

pavāritasikkhāpadaṃ

54. yā pana bhikkhunī nimantitā vā pavāritā vā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

kulamaccharinīsikkhāpadaṃ

55. yā pana bhikkhunī kulamaccharinī assa, pācittiyaṃ|

abhikkhukāvāsasikkhāpadaṃ

56. yā pana bhikkhunī abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

apavāraṇāsikkhāpadaṃ

57. yā pana bhikkhunī vassaṃvuṭṭhā ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi na pavāraiyya diṭṭhaina vā sutaina vā parisaṅkāya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

auvādasikkhāpadaṃ

58. yā pana bhikkhunī auvādāya vā saṃvāsāya vā na gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

auvādūpasaṅkamanasikkhāpadaṃ

59. anvaddhamāsaṃ bhikkhuniyā bhikkhusaṅghatau dvai dhammā pac‍cāsīsitabbā upausathapucchakañ‍ca auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā pācittiyaṃ|

pasākhaijātasikkhāpadaṃ

60. yā pana bhikkhunī pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ vā rudhitaṃ vā anapalaukaitvā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaiyya vā  phālāpaiyya vā dhauvāpaiyya vā ālimpāpaiyya vā bandhāpaiyya vā maucāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

ārāmavaggau chaṭṭhau|

gabbhinīsikkhāpadaṃ

61. yā  pana bhikkhunī gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

pāyantīsikkhāpadaṃ

62. yā pana bhikkhunī pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasikkhamānasikkhāpadaṃ

63. yā pana bhikkhunī dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasikkhamānasikkhāpadaṃ

64. yā pana bhikkhunī dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamagihigatasikkhāpadaṃ

65. yā pana bhikkhunī ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyagihigatasikkhāpadaṃ

66. yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

tatiyagihigatasikkhāpadaṃ

67. yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasahajīvinīsikkhāpadaṃ

68. yā pana bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhaiyya na anuggaṇhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

pavattinīnānubandhanasikkhāpadaṃ

69. yā pana bhikkhunī vuṭṭhāpitaṃ pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasahajīvinīsikkhāpadaṃ

70. yā  pana bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaiyya na vūpakāsāpaiyya antamasau chappañ‍cayaujanānipi, pācittiyaṃ|

gabbhinivaggau sattamau|

paṭhamakumāribhūtasikkhāpadaṃ

71. yā pana bhikkhunī ūnavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyakumāribhūtasikkhāpadaṃ

72. yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

tatiyakumāribhūtasikkhāpadaṃ

73. yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

ūnadvādasavassasikkhāpadaṃ

74. yā pana bhikkhunī ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassasikkhāpadaṃ

75. yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

khiyyanadhammasikkhāpadaṃ

76. yā pana bhikkhunī ‘‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’’ti vuc‍camānā ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā sā pacchā khiyyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasikkhamānanavuṭṭhāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

77. yā pana bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ  vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā sā pacchā anantarāyikinī naiva vuṭṭhāpaiyya, na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasikkhamānanavuṭṭhāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

78. yā  pana bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ ‘‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ, ayyai, dvai vassāni anubandhissasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā sā pacchā anantarāyikinī naiva vuṭṭhāpaiyya, na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

saukāvāsasikkhāpadaṃ

79. yā pana bhikkhunī purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

ananuñ‍ñātasikkhāpadaṃ

80. yā pana bhikkhunī mātāpitūhi vā sāmikaina vā ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

pārivāsikasikkhāpadaṃ

81. yā pana bhikkhunī pārivāsikachandadānaina sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

anuvassasikkhāpadaṃ

82. yā pana bhikkhunī anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

aikavassasikkhāpadaṃ

83. yā pana bhikkhunī aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

kumāribhūtavaggau aṭṭhamau|

chattupāhanasikkhāpadaṃ

84. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā chattupāhanaṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

yānasikkhāpadaṃ

85. yā  pana bhikkhunī agilānā yānaina yāyaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

saṅghāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

86. yā pana bhikkhunī saṅghāṇiṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

itthālaṅkārasikkhāpadaṃ

87. yā pana bhikkhunī itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

gandhavaṇṇakasikkhāpadaṃ

88. yā  pana bhikkhunī gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

vāsitakasikkhāpadaṃ

89. yā pana bhikkhunī vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

bhikkhuniummaddāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

90. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaiyya vā parimaddāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

sikkhamānaummaddāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

91. yā pana bhikkhunī sikkhamānāya ummaddāpaiyya vā parimaddāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

sāmaṇairīummaddāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

92. yā pana bhikkhunī sāmaṇairiyā ummaddāpaiyya vā parimaddāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

gihiniummaddāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

93. yā pana bhikkhunī gihiniyā ummaddāpaiyya vā parimaddāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

anāpucchāsikkhāpadaṃ

94. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

pañhāpucchanasikkhāpadaṃ

95. yā  pana bhikkhunī anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

asaṃkac‍cikasikkhāpadaṃ

96. yā pana bhikkhunī asaṃkac‍cikā gāmaṃ pavisaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

chattupāhanavaggau navamau|

musāvādasikkhāpadaṃ

97. sampajānamusāvādai  pācittiyaṃ|

aumasavādasikkhāpadaṃ

98. aumasavādai pācittiyaṃ|

paisuñ‍ñasikkhāpadaṃ

99. bhikkhunipaisuñ‍ñai pācittiyaṃ|

padasaudhammasikkhāpadaṃ

100. yā pana bhikkhunī anupasampan‍naṃ padasau dhammaṃ vācaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasahasaiyyasikkhāpadaṃ

101. yā pana bhikkhunī anupasampan‍nāya uttaridirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasahasaiyyasikkhāpadaṃ

102. yā pana bhikkhunī purisaina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dhammadaisanāsikkhāpadaṃ

103. yā pana bhikkhunī purisassa uttarichappañ‍cavācāhi dhammaṃ daisaiyya añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñunā itthiviggahaina, pācittiyaṃ|

bhūtāraucanasikkhāpadaṃ

104. yā  pana bhikkhunī anupasampan‍nāya uttarimanussadhammaṃ āraucaiyya, bhūtasmiṃ pācittiyaṃ|

duṭṭhul‍lāraucanasikkhāpadaṃ

105. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ anupasampan‍nāya āraucaiyya añ‍ñatra bhikkhunisammutiyā, pācittiyaṃ|

pathavīkhaṇanasikkhāpadaṃ

106. yā pana bhikkhunī pathaviṃ khaṇaiyya vā khaṇāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

musāvādavaggau dasamau|

bhūtagāmasikkhāpadaṃ

107. bhūtagāmapātabyatāya  pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñavādakasikkhāpadaṃ

108. añ‍ñavādakai, vihaisakai pācittiyaṃ|

ujjhāpanakasikkhāpadaṃ

109. ujjhāpanakai, khiyyanakai pācittiyaṃ|

paṭhamasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ

110. yā pana bhikkhunī saṅghikaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā bhisiṃ vā kaucchaṃ vā ajjhaukāsai santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantī naiva uddharaiyya, na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dutiyasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ

111. yā pana bhikkhunī saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantī naiva uddharaiyya, na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

anupakhaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

112. yā  pana bhikkhunī saṅghikai vihārai jānaṃ pubbupagataṃ bhikkhuniṃ anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaiyya ‘‘yassā sambādhau bhavissati, sā pak‍kamissatī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

nik‍kaḍḍhanasikkhāpadaṃ

113. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniṃ kupitā anattamanā saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhaiyya vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

vaihāsakuṭisikkhāpadaṃ

114. yā pana bhikkhunī saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

mahal‍lakavihārasikkhāpadaṃ

115. mahal‍lakaṃ pana bhikkhuniyā vihāraṃ kārayamānāya yāva dvārakausā aggaḷaṭṭhapanāya, ālaukasandhiparikammāya dvatticchadanassa pariyāyaṃ appaharitai ṭhitāya adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari appaharitaipi ṭhitā adhiṭṭhahaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ

116. yā  pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍caiyya vā siñ‍cāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

bhūtagāmavaggau aikādasamau|

āvasathapiṇḍasikkhāpadaṃ

117. agilānāya bhikkhuniyā aikau āvasathapiṇḍau bhuñ‍jitabbau| tatau cai uttari bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

gaṇabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

118. gaṇabhaujanai añ‍ñatra samayā pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, nāvābhiruhanasamayau, mahāsamayau, samaṇabhattasamayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

kāṇamātusikkhāpadaṃ

119. bhikkhuniṃ panaiva kulaṃ upagataṃ pūvaihi vā manthaihi vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya, ākaṅkhamānāya bhikkhuniyā dvattipattapūrā paṭiggahaitabbā| tatau cai uttari paṭiggaṇhaiyya, pācittiyaṃ| dvattipattapūrai paṭiggahaitvā tatau nīharitvā bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvibhajitabbaṃ, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

vikālabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

120. yā pana bhikkhunī vikālai khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

san‍nidhikārakasikkhāpadaṃ

121. yā pana bhikkhunī san‍nidhikārakaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ|

dantapaunasikkhāpadaṃ

122. yā pana bhikkhunī adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharaiyya añ‍ñatra udakadantapaunā, pācittiyaṃ|

uyyaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

123. yā  pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniṃ ‘‘aihāyyai, gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti tassā dāpaitvā vā adāpaitvā vā uyyaujaiyya ‘‘gacchāyyai, na mai tayā saddhiṃ kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hauti, aikikāya mai kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hautī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

sabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

124. yā pana bhikkhunī sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

rahaupaṭicchan‍nasikkhāpadaṃ

125. yā  pana bhikkhunī purisaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

rahaunisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

126. yā pana bhikkhunī purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

bhaujanavaggau dvādasamau|

cārittasikkhāpadaṃ

127. yā pana bhikkhunī nimantitā sabhattā samānā santiṃ bhikkhuniṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

mahānāmasikkhāpadaṃ

128. agilānāya bhikkhuniyā catumāsappac‍cayapavāraṇā sāditabbā añ‍ñatra punapavāraṇāya, añ‍ñatra nic‍capavāraṇāya| tatau cai uttari sādiyaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

uyyuttasaināsikkhāpadaṃ

129. yā pana bhikkhunī uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchaiyya añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, pācittiyaṃ|

saināvāsasikkhāpadaṃ

130. siyā  ca tassā bhikkhuniyā kaucidaiva pac‍cayau sainaṃ gamanāya, dirattatirattaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā saināya vasitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari vasaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

uyyaudhikasikkhāpadaṃ

131. dirattatirattaṃ cai bhikkhunī saināya vasamānā uyyaudhikaṃ vā balaggaṃ vā sainābyūhaṃ vā anīkadassanaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

surāpānasikkhāpadaṃ

132. surāmairayapānai  pācittiyaṃ|

aṅgulipataudakasikkhāpadaṃ

133. aṅgulipataudakai pācittiyaṃ|

hasadhammasikkhāpadaṃ

134. udakai hasadhammai pācittiyaṃ|

anādariyasikkhāpadaṃ

135. anādariyai pācittiyaṃ|

bhiṃsāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

136. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniṃ bhiṃsāpaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

cārittavaggau tairasamau|

jautisikkhāpadaṃ

137. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā visibbanāpaikkhā jautiṃ samādahaiyya vā samādahāpaiyya vā añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, pācittiyaṃ|

nahānasikkhāpadaṃ

138. yā pana bhikkhunī aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau ‘‘diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhāna’’nti ‘‘vassānassa paṭhamau māsau’’ ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau, pariḷāhasamayau, gilānasamayau, kammasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, vātavuṭṭhisamayau, ayaṃ tattha samayau|

dubbaṇṇakaraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

139. navaṃ  pana bhikkhuniyā cīvaralābhāya tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ  ādātabbaṃ nīlaṃ vā kaddamaṃ vā kāḷasāmaṃ vā| anādā cai bhikkhunī tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

vikappanasikkhāpadaṃ

140. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā sikkhamānāya vā sāmaṇairassa vā sāmaṇairiyā vā sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā apac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

apanidhāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

141. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaiyya vā apanidhāpaiyya vā antamasau hasāpaikkhāpi, pācittiyaṃ|

sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ

142. yā pana bhikkhunī sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ

143. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

uk‍kauṭanasikkhāpadaṃ

144. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

thaiyyasatthasikkhāpadaṃ

145. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya antamasau gāmantarampi, pācittiyaṃ|

ariṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

146. yā pana bhikkhunī aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyai aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na  hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya, anaikapariyāyaināyyai antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau  antarāyāyā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

jautivaggau cuddasamau|

ukkhittasambhaugasikkhāpadaṃ

147. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ tathāvādiniyā bhikkhuniyā akaṭānudhammāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhāya saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jaiyya vā, saṃvasaiyya vā, saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

kaṇṭakasikkhāpadaṃ

148. samaṇuddaisāpi cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| sā samaṇuddaisā bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘māyyai, samaṇuddaisai aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya, anaikapariyāyaināyyai, samaṇuddaisai antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti| aivañ‍ca sā samaṇuddaisā bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā samaṇuddaisā bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā ‘‘aj‍jataggai tai, ayyai, samaṇuddaisai na caiva sau bhagavā satthā apadisitabbau, yampi cañ‍ñā samaṇuddaisā labhanti bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ dirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ, sāpi tai natthi, cara pirai, vinassā’’ti| yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaiyya vā, upaṭṭhāpaiyya vā, sambhuñ‍jaiyya vā, saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sahadhammikasikkhāpadaṃ

149. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, ayyai, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi, yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuniṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti, pācittiyaṃ| sikkhamānāya, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā añ‍ñātabbaṃ paripucchitabbaṃ paripañhitabbaṃ, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

vilaikhanasikkhāpadaṃ

150. yā  pana bhikkhunī pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘kiṃ panimaihi khuddānukhuddakaihi sikkhāpadaihi uddiṭṭhaihi, yāvadaiva kuk‍kuc‍cāya vihaisāya vilaikhāya saṃvattantī’’ti, sikkhāpadavivaṇṇakai pācittiyaṃ|

mauhanasikkhāpadaṃ

151. yā pana bhikkhunī anvaddhamāsaṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘idānaiva khau ahaṃ, ayyai, jānāmi ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’’ti, tañ‍cai bhikkhuniṃ añ‍ñā bhikkhuniyau jānaiyyuṃ nisin‍napubbaṃ imāya bhikkhuniyā dvattikkhattuṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānai, kau pana vādau bhiyyau, na ca tassā bhikkhuniyā añ‍ñāṇakaina mutti atthi, yañ‍ca tattha āpattiṃ āpan‍nā, tañ‍ca yathādhammau kāraitabbau, uttari cassā mauhau āraupaitabbau ‘‘tassā tai, ayyai, alābhā, tassā tai dul‍laddhaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānai na sādhukaṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi karausī’’ti, idaṃ tasmiṃ mauhanakai pācittiyaṃ|

pahārasikkhāpadaṃ

152. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā kupitā anattamanā pahāraṃ dadaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

talasattikasikkhāpadaṃ

153. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā kupitā anattamanā talasattikaṃ uggiraiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

amūlakasikkhāpadaṃ

154. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ

155. yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahaiyya ‘‘itissā muhuttampi aphāsu bhavissatī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

upassuti sikkhāpadaṃ

156. yā  pana bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhaiyya ‘‘yaṃ imā bhaṇissanti, taṃ saussāmī’’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiyaṃ|

diṭṭhivaggau pan‍narasamau|

kammappaṭibāhanasikkhāpadaṃ

157. yā pana bhikkhunī dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

chandaṃadatvāgamanasikkhāpadaṃ

158. yā pana bhikkhunī saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

dubbalasikkhāpadaṃ

159. yā pana bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘‘yathāsanthutaṃ bhikkhuniyau saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇāmaintī’’ti, pācittiyaṃ|

pariṇāmanasikkhāpadaṃ

160. yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaiyya, pācittiyaṃ|

ratanasikkhāpadaṃ

161. yā pana bhikkhunī ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā añ‍ñatra ajjhārāmā vā ajjhāvasathā vā uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ| ratanaṃ vā pana bhikkhuniyā ratanasammataṃ vā ajjhārāmai vā ajjhāvasathai vā uggahaitvā vā uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipitabbaṃ ‘‘yassa bhavissati, sau harissatī’’ti, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

sūcigharasikkhāpadaṃ

162. yā  pana bhikkhunī aṭṭhimayaṃ vā dantamayaṃ vā visāṇamayaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ kārāpaiyya, bhaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

mañ‍capīṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

163. navaṃ pana bhikkhuniyā mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā kārayamānāya aṭṭhaṅgulapādakaṃ kāraitabbaṃ sugataṅgulaina añ‍ñatra haiṭṭhimāya aṭaniyā| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

tūlaunaddhasikkhāpadaṃ

164. yā pana bhikkhunī mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaiyya, uddālanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kaṇḍuppaṭicchādisikkhāpadaṃ

165. kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiṃ pana bhikkhuniyā kārayamānāya pamāṇikā kāraitabbā, tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ, dīghasau catassau vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ dvai vidatthiyau| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nandasikkhāpadaṃ

166. yā pana bhikkhunī sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ kārāpaiyya, atiraikaṃ vā, chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ| tatridaṃ sugatassa sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ, dīghasau nava vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ cha vidatthiyau, idaṃ sugatassa sugatacīvarappamāṇanti|

dhammikavaggau sauḷasamau|

uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, chasaṭṭhisatā pācittiyā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau, pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

pāṭidaisanīyā

imai khau panāyyāyau aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

sappiviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

1. yā  pana bhikkhunī agilānā sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā ‘‘gārayhaṃ, ayyai, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’ti|

tailaviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

2. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā tailaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya…pai॰… taṃ paṭidaisaimīti|

madhuviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

3. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā madhuṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya…pai॰… taṃ paṭidaisaimīti|

phāṇitaviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

4. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā phāṇitaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya…pai॰… taṃ paṭidaisaimīti|

macchaviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

5. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā macchaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya…pai॰… taṃ paṭidaisaimīti|

maṃsaviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

6. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā maṃsaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya…pai॰… taṃ paṭidaisaimīti|

khīraviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

7. yā  pana bhikkhunī agilānā khīraṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya…pai॰… taṃ paṭidaisaimīti|

dadhiviñ‍ñāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

8. yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā ‘‘gārayhaṃ, ayyai, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’ti|

uddiṭṭhā  khau, ayyāyau, aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau, pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pāṭidaisanīyā niṭṭhitā|

saikhiyā

imai khau panāyyāyau, saikhiyā dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

parimaṇḍalasikkhāpadaṃ

1. parimaṇḍalaṃ nivāsaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

2. parimaṇḍalaṃ pārupissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

suppaṭicchan‍nasikkhāpadaṃ

3. suppaṭicchan‍nā antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

4. suppaṭicchan‍nā antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

susaṃvutasikkhāpadaṃ

5. susaṃvutā  antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

6. susaṃvutā antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

aukkhittacakkhusikkhāpadaṃ

7. aukkhittacakkhunī antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

8. aukkhittacakkhunī antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ukkhittakasikkhāpadaṃ

9. na ukkhittakāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

10. na ukkhittakāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

parimaṇḍalavaggau paṭhamau|

uj‍jagghikasikkhāpadaṃ

11. na  uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

12. na uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uc‍casaddasikkhāpadaṃ

13. appasaddā antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

14. appasaddā antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

kāyappacālakasikkhāpadaṃ

15. na kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

16. na  kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

bāhuppacālakasikkhāpadaṃ

17. na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

18. na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sīsappacālakasikkhāpadaṃ

19. na sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

20. na sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uj‍jagghikavaggau dutiyau|

khambhakatasikkhāpadaṃ

21. na khambhakatā antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

22. na khambhakatā antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

auguṇṭhitasikkhāpadaṃ

23. na auguṇṭhitā antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

24. na auguṇṭhitā antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uk‍kuṭikasikkhāpadaṃ

25. na uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pal‍latthikasikkhāpadaṃ

26. na  pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sak‍kac‍capaṭiggahaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

27. sak‍kac‍caṃ  piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pattasañ‍ñinīpaṭiggahaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

28. pattasañ‍ñinī piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

samasūpakapaṭiggahaṇasikkhāpadaṃ

29. samasūpakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

samatittikasikkhāpadaṃ

30. samatittikaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

khambhakatavaggau tatiyau|

sak‍kac‍cabhuñ‍janasikkhāpadaṃ

31. sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pattasañ‍ñinībhuñ‍janasikkhāpadaṃ

32. pattasañ‍ñinī piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sapadānasikkhāpadaṃ

33. sapadānaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

samasūpakasikkhāpadaṃ

34. samasūpakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

na thūpakatasikkhāpadaṃ

35. na thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

audanappaṭicchādanasikkhāpadaṃ

36. na sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaissāmi bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāyāti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sūpaudanaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ

37. na  sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānā attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ujjhānasañ‍ñinīsikkhāpadaṃ

38. na ujjhānasañ‍ñinī paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

kabaḷasikkhāpadaṃ

39. nātimahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ālaupasikkhāpadaṃ

40. parimaṇḍalaṃ ālaupaṃ karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sak‍kac‍cavaggau catutthau|

anāhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

41. na anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivarissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

bhuñ‍jamānasikkhāpadaṃ

42. na bhuñ‍jamānā sabbahatthaṃ mukhai pakkhipissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sakabaḷasikkhāpadaṃ

43. na sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāharissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

piṇḍukkhaipakasikkhāpadaṃ

44. na piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

kabaḷāvacchaidakasikkhāpadaṃ

45. na kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

avagaṇḍakārakasikkhāpadaṃ

46. na  avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

hatthaniddhunakasikkhāpadaṃ

47. na hatthaniddhunakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sitthāvakārakasikkhāpadaṃ

48. na sitthāvakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

jivhānicchārakasikkhāpadaṃ

49. na  jivhānicchārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

capucapukārakasikkhāpadaṃ

50. na capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

kabaḷavaggau pañ‍camau|

surusurukārakasikkhāpadaṃ

51. na surusurukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

hatthanil‍laihakasikkhāpadaṃ

52. na hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pattanil‍laihakasikkhāpadaṃ

53. na pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

auṭṭhanil‍laihakasikkhāpadaṃ

54. na auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sāmisasikkhāpadaṃ

55. na  sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sasitthakasikkhāpadaṃ

56. na sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

chattapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

57. na chattapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

daṇḍapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

58. na daṇḍapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

satthapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

59. na  satthapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

āvudhapāṇisikkhāpadaṃ

60. na āvudhapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

surusuruvaggau chaṭṭhau|

pādukasikkhāpadaṃ

61. na pādukāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

upāhanasikkhāpadaṃ

62. na upāhanāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

yānasikkhāpadaṃ

63. na yānagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

sayanasikkhāpadaṃ

64. na sayanagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pal‍latthikasikkhāpadaṃ

65. na pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

vaiṭhitasikkhāpadaṃ

66. na  vaiṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

auguṇṭhitasikkhāpadaṃ

67. na auguṇṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

chamāsikkhāpadaṃ

68. na  chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

nīcāsanasikkhāpadaṃ

69. na nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ṭhitāsikkhāpadaṃ

70. na ṭhitā nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pacchataugacchantīsikkhāpadaṃ

71. na pacchatau gacchantī puratau gacchantassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

uppathainagacchantīsikkhāpadaṃ

72. na uppathaina gacchantī pathaina gacchantassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

ṭhitāuc‍cārasikkhāpadaṃ

73. na ṭhitā agilānā uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

haritaiuc‍cārasikkhāpadaṃ

74. na  haritai agilānā uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

udakaiuc‍cārasikkhāpadaṃ

75. na udakai agilānā uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā|

pādukavaggau sattamau|

uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, saikhiyā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau, pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

saikhiyā niṭṭhitā|

adhikaraṇasamathā

imai  khau panāyyāyau, satta adhikaraṇasamathā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāya sammukhāvinayau dātabbau|

sativinayau dātabbau|

amūḷhavinayau dātabbau|

paṭiñ‍ñāya kāraitabbaṃ|

yaibhuyyasikā|

tassapāpiyasikā|

tiṇavatthārakauti|

uddiṭṭhā khau ayyāyau satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

adhikaraṇasamathā niṭṭhitā|

uddiṭṭhaṃ khau ayyāyau nidānaṃ,

uddiṭṭhā aṭṭha pārājikā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā sattarasa saṅghādisaisā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā chasaṭṭhi satā pācittiyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā saikhiyā dhammā,

uddiṭṭhā satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā, aittakaṃ tassa bhagavatau suttāgataṃ suttapariyāpan‍naṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchati, tattha sabbāhaiva samaggāhi sammaudamānāhi avivadamānāhi sikkhitabbanti|

vitthāruddaisau catutthau|

bhikkhunipātimaukkhaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

kaṅkhāvitaraṇī-aṭṭhakathā

ganthārambhakathā

buddhaṃ  dhammañ‍ca saṅghañ‍ca, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

vanditvā vandanāmāna, pūjāsak‍kārabhājanaṃ||

thairavaṃsappadīpānaṃ, thirānaṃ vinayak‍kamai|

pubbācariyasīhānaṃ, namau katvā katañ‍jalī||

pāmaukkhaṃ anavaj‍jānaṃ, dhammānaṃ yaṃ mahaisinā|

mukhaṃ maukkhappavaisāya, pātimaukkhaṃ pakāsitaṃ||

sūrataina  nivātaina, sucisal‍laikhavuttinā|

vinayācārayuttaina, sauṇatthairaina yācitau||

tattha sañ‍jātakaṅkhānaṃ, bhikkhūnaṃ tassa vaṇṇanaṃ|

kaṅkhāvitaraṇatthāya, paripuṇṇavinicchayaṃ||

mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ, vācanāmagganissitaṃ|

vattayissāmi nāmaina, kaṅkhāvitaraṇiṃ subhanti||

nidānavaṇṇanā

tattha  pātimaukkhanti paatimaukkhaṃ atipamaukkhaṃ atisaiṭṭhaṃ atiuttamanti atthau| iti iminā vacanatthaina aikavidhampi sīlaganthabhaidatau duvidhaṃ hauti| tathā hi ‘‘pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharatī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.69; 3.75; vibha॰ 508) ca ‘‘ādimaitaṃ mukhamaitaṃ pamukhamaitaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, taina vuc‍cati pātimaukkha’’nti (mahāva॰ 135) ca ādīsu sīlaṃ pātimaukkhanti vuc‍cati, ‘‘ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni hauntī’’tiādīsu (pāci॰ 147; a॰ ni॰ 8.52; 10.33) ganthau pātimaukkhanti vuc‍cati| tattha sīlaṃ yau naṃ pāti rakkhati, taṃ maukkhaiti maucayati āpāyikādīhi dukkhaihi, attānuvādādīhi vā bhayaihīti pātimaukkhaṃ| ganthau pana tassa pātimaukkhassa jautakattā pātimaukkhanti vuc‍cati| ādimhi pana vuttau vacanatthau ubhin‍nampi sādhāraṇau hauti|

tatthāyaṃ vaṇṇanā sīlapātimaukkhassāpi yuj‍jati ganthapātimaukkhassāpi, ganthai hi vaṇṇitai tassa atthau vaṇṇitauva hauti| taṃ panaitaṃ ganthapātimaukkhaṃ bhikkhupātimaukkhaṃ bhikkhunipātimaukkhanti duvidhaṃ hauti| tattha ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau’’tiādikaṃ (mahāva॰ 134) pañ‍cahi uddaisaparicchaidaihi vavatthitaṃ bhikkhupātimaukkhaṃ, ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau’’tiādikaṃ catūhi uddaisaparicchaidaihi vavatthitaṃ bhikkhunipātimaukkhaṃ| tattha bhikkhupātimaukkhai pañ‍ca uddaisā nāma nidānuddaisau, pārājikuddaisau, saṅghādisaisuddaisau, aniyatuddaisau, vitthāruddaisauti| tattha nidānuddaisau tāva ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau…pai॰… āvikatā hissa phāsu hauti, tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, dutiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, tatiyampi pucchāmi, kac‍cittha parisuddhā, parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti vatvā ‘‘uddiṭṭhaṃ khau āyasmantau nidāna’’ntiādinā nayaina avasaisai sutaina sāvitai uddiṭṭhau hauti| pārājikuddaisādīnaṃ paricchaidā nidānassa āditau paṭṭhāya pārājikādīni ausāpaitvā yaujaitabbā| vitthārau vitthārauyaiva| ‘‘avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabba’’nti (mahāva॰ 150; pari॰ 325) vacanatau pana pārājikuddaisādīsu yasmiṃ vippakatai antarāyau uppaj‍jati, taina saddhiṃ avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ| nidānuddaisai pana aniṭṭhitai sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ nāma natthi| bhikkhunipātimaukkhai pana aniyatuddaisau parihāyati, saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva| aivamaitaisaṃ pañ‍cahi caiva catūhi ca uddaisaparicchaidaihi vavatthitānaṃ dvin‍nampi pātimaukkhānaṃ ayaṃ vaṇṇanā bhavissati| yasmā panaittha bhikkhupātimaukkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ, tasmā tassa tāva vaṇṇanatthamidaṃ vuc‍cati|

‘‘suṇātu mai’’tiādīnaṃ, padānaṃ atthanicchayaṃ|

bhikkhavau sīlasampan‍nā, sikkhākāmā suṇantu maiti||

aittha hi suṇātūtiidaṃ savanāṇattivacanaṃ| maiti yau sāvaiti, tassa attaniddaisavacanaṃ| bhantaiti sagāravasappatissavacanaṃ| saṅghauti puggalasamūhavacanaṃ| sabbamaiva caitaṃ pātimaukkhuddaisakaina paṭhamaṃ vattabbavacanaṃ| bhagavatā hi pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ anujānantaina rājagahai vuttaṃ, tasmā yau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati, taina sacai saṅghatthairau hauti, ‘‘āvusau’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai navakatarau hauti, pāḷiyaṃ (mahāva॰ 134) āgatanayainaiva ‘‘bhantai’’ti vattabbaṃ| saṅghatthairau vā hi pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyya ‘‘thairādhikaṃ pātimaukkha’’ntivacanatau (mahāva॰ 154), navakatarau vā ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yau tattha bhikkhu byattau paṭibalau, tassādhaiyyaṃ pātimaukkha’’ntivacanatau (mahāva॰ 155)|

‘‘saṅghau’’tiiminā pana padaina kiñ‍cāpi avisaisatau puggalasamūhau vuttau, atha khau sau dakkhiṇaiyyasaṅghau, sammutisaṅghau cāti duvidhau hauti| tattha dakkhiṇaiyyasaṅghauti aṭṭha ariyapuggalasamūhau vuc‍cati| sammutisaṅghauti avisaisaina bhikkhusamūhau, sau idha adhippaitau| sau  panaisa kammavasaina pañ‍cavidhau (mahāva॰ 388) hauti – catuvaggau pañ‍cavaggau dasavaggau vīsativaggau atiraikavīsativaggauti| tattha catuvaggaina ṭhapaitvā upasampadapavāraṇaabbhānāni sabbaṃ  saṅghakammaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭati| pañ‍cavaggaina ṭhapaitvā majjhimaisu janapadaisu upasampadañ‍ca abbhānakammañ‍ca sabbaṃ saṅghakammaṃ kātuṃ  vaṭṭati| dasavaggaina abbhānakammamattaṃ ṭhapaitvā sabbaṃ saṅghakammaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭati| vīsativaggaina na kiñ‍ci saṅghakammaṃ kātuṃ na vaṭṭati, tathā atiraikavīsativaggaina|sau pana catuvaggādinā saṅghaina kattabbaṃ kammaṃ ūnakataraina kātuṃ na vaṭṭati, atiraikaina pana vaṭṭatīti dassanatthaṃ vuttau| imasmiṃ panatthai catuvaggaṃ upādāya sabbaupi sammutisaṅghau adhippaitau|

aj‍jupausathauti aj‍ja upausathadivasau, aitaina anupausathadivasaṃ paṭikkhipati| pan‍narasauti iminā añ‍ñaṃ upausathadivasaṃ paṭikkhipati| divasavasaina hi tayau upausathā cātuddasikau pan‍narasikau sāmaggiupausathauti, aivaṃ tayau upausathā vuttā| tattha haimantagimhavassānānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ utūnaṃ tatiyasattamapakkhaisu dvai dvai katvā cha cātuddasikā, avasaisā aṭṭhārasa pan‍narasikāti aivaṃ aikasaṃvaccharai catuvīsati upausathā, idaṃ tāva pakaticārittaṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sakiṃ pakkhassa cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitu’’nti (mahāva॰ 136) vacanatau pana ‘‘āgantukaihi āvāsikānaṃ anuvattitabba’’ntiādivacanatau (mahāva॰ 178) ca tathārūpapac‍cayai sati añ‍ñasmimpi cātuddasai upausathaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭati| purimavassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ pana pubbakattikapuṇṇamā vā, taisaṃyaiva sacai bhaṇḍanakārakaihi upaddutā pavāraṇaṃ pac‍cuk‍kaḍḍhanti, atha pubbakattikamāsassa kāḷapakkhacātuddasau vā, pacchimakattikapuṇṇamā vā, pacchimavassaṃvuṭṭhānañ‍ca pacchimakattikapuṇṇamā aivāti imai tayau pavāraṇādivasāpi haunti, idampi pakaticārittamaiva| tathārūpapac‍cayai pana sati dvin‍naṃ kattikapuṇṇamānaṃ purimaisu cātuddasaisupi pavāraṇaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭati| yadā pana kausambakakkhandhakai (mahāva॰ 451) āgatanayaina bhin‍nai saṅghai ausāritai tasmiṃ bhikkhusmiṃ saṅghau tassa vatthussa vūpasamāya saṅghassa sāmaggiṃ karauti, tadā ‘‘tāvadaiva upausathau kātabbau, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabba’’nti (mahāva॰ 475) vacanatau ṭhapaitvā cātuddasapan‍narasai, añ‍ñaupi yau kauci divasau sāmaggiupausathadivasau nāma hauti, purimavassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ pana kattikamāsabbhantarai ayamaiva sāmaggipavāraṇādivasau nāma hauti| iti imaisu tīsu divasaisu ‘‘pan‍narasau’’tiiminā añ‍ñaṃ upausathadivasaṃ paṭikkhipati| tasmā yvāyaṃ ‘‘aj‍jupausathau’’tivacanaina  anupausathadivasau  paṭikkhittau, tasmiṃ upausathau na kātabbauyaiva| yau panāyaṃ añ‍ñau upausathadivasau, tasmiṃ upausathau kātabbau| karauntaina pana sacai cātuddasikau hauti, ‘‘aj‍jupausathau cātuddasau’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai pan‍narasikau hauti, ‘‘aj‍jupausathau pan‍narasau’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai sāmaggiupausathau hauti, ‘‘aj‍jupausathau sāmaggī’’ti vattabbaṃ|

yadi  saṅghassa pattakal‍lanti aittha pattau kālau imassa kammassāti pattakālaṃ, pattakālamaiva pattakal‍laṃ| tadaitaṃ idha catūhi aṅgaihi saṅgahitaṃ| yathāhu aṭṭhakathācariyā –

‘‘upausathau yāvatikā ca bhikkhū kammappattā,

sabhāgāpattiyau ca na vij‍janti|

vaj‍janīyā ca puggalā tasmiṃ na haunti,

‘pattakal‍la’nti vuc‍catī’’ti|| (mahāva॰ aṭṭha॰ 168)|

tattha upausathauti tīsu upausathadivasaisu añ‍ñataraupausathadivasau| tasmiñhi sati idaṃ saṅghassa upausathakammaṃ pattakal‍laṃ nāma hauti, nāsati| yathāha ‘‘na ca, bhikkhavai, anupausathai upausathau kātabbau, yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti (mahāva॰ 183)|

yāvatikā ca bhikkhū kammappattāti yattakā bhikkhū tassa upausathakammassa pattā yuttā anurūpā, sabbantimaina paricchaidaina cattārau pakatattā, tai ca khau hatthapāsaṃ avijahitvā aikasīmāyaṃ ṭhitā|

sīmā ca nāmaisā baddhasīmā abaddhasīmāti duvidhā hauti| tattha aikādasa vipattisīmāyau atik‍kamitvā tividhasampattiyuttā nimittaina nimittaṃ sambandhitvā sammatā sīmā baddhasīmā nāma, ‘‘atikhuddakā, atimahatī, khaṇḍanimittā, chāyānimittā, animittā, bahisīmai ṭhitā sammatā, nadiyā sammatā, samuddai sammatā, jātassarai sammatā, sīmāya sīmaṃ sambhindantaina sammatā, sīmāya sīmaṃ ajjhauttharantaina sammatā’’ti imā hi ‘‘aikādasahi ākāraihi sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍jantī’’ti (pari॰ 486) vacanatau aikādasa vipattisīmāyau nāma| tattha atikhuddakā nāma yattha aikavīsati bhikkhū nisīdituṃ na sak‍kaunti| atimahatī nāma yā antamasau kaisaggamattaināpi tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kamitvā sammatā| khaṇḍanimittā nāma aghaṭitanimittā vuc‍cati, puratthimāya disāya nimittaṃ kittaitvā anuk‍kamainaiva dakkhiṇāya pacchimāya uttarāya disāya kittaitvā puna puratthimāya disāya pubbakittitaṃ  paṭikittaitvā ṭhapaituṃ vaṭṭati, aivaṃ akkhaṇḍanimittā hauti| sacai pana anuk‍kamaina āharitvā uttarāya disāya nimittaṃ kittaitvā tatthaiva ṭhapaiti, khaṇḍanimittā nāma hauti| aparāpi khaṇḍanimittā nāma yā animittupagaṃ tacasārarukkhaṃ vā khāṇukaṃ vā paṃsupuñ‍javālukapuñ‍jānaṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ antarā aikaṃ nimittaṃ katvā sammatā| chāyānimittā nāma  pabbatacchāyādīnaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci chāyaṃ nimittaṃ katvā sammatā| animittā nāma sabbaina sabbaṃ nimittāni akittaitvā sammatā| bahisīmai ṭhitasammatā nāma nimittāni kittaitvā nimittānaṃ bahiṭhitaina sammatā| nadiyā samuddai jātassarai sammatā nāma aitaisu nadiādīsu sammatā| sā hi aivaṃ sammatāpi ‘‘sabbā, bhikkhavai, nadī asīmā, sabbau samuddau asīmau, sabbau jātassarau asīmau’’ti (mahāva॰ 147) vacanatau asammatāva hauti| sīmāya sīmaṃ sambhindantaina sammatā nāma attanau sīmāya paraisaṃ sīmaṃ sambhindantaina sammatā| sacai hi paurāṇakassa vihārassa puratthimāya disāya ambau caiva jambū cāti dvai rukkhā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaviṭapā haunti, taisu ambassa pacchimadisābhāgai jambū, vihārasīmā ca jambuṃ antau katvā ambaṃ kittaitvā baddhā hauti, atha pacchā tassa vihārassa puratthimāya disāya vihārai katai sīmaṃ bandhantā bhikkhū taṃ ambaṃ antau katvā jambuṃ kittaitvā bandhanti, sīmāya sīmaṃ sambhin‍nā hauti| sīmāya sīmaṃ ajjhauttharantaina sammatā nāma attanau sīmāya paraisaṃ sīmaṃ ajjhauttharantaina sammatā| sacai hi paraisaṃ baddhasīmaṃ sakalaṃ vā tassā padaisaṃ vā antau katvā attanau sīmaṃ samman‍nati, sīmāya sīmaṃ ajjhauttharitā nāma hauti| iti imā aikādasa vipattisīmāyau atik‍kamitvā sammatā|

tividhasampattiyuttāti nimittasampattiyā parisāsampattiyā kammavācāsampattiyā ca yuttā| tattha nimittasampattiyā yuttā nāma pabbatanimittaṃ, pāsāṇanimittaṃ, vananimittaṃ, rukkhanimittaṃ, magganimittaṃ, vammikanimittaṃ, nadinimittaṃ, udakanimittanti aivaṃ vuttaisu aṭṭhasu nimittaisu tasmiṃ tasmiṃ disābhāgai yathāladdhāni  nimittupagāni nimittāni ‘‘puratthimāya disāya kiṃ nimittaṃ, pabbatau, bhantai, aisau pabbatau nimitta’’ntiādinā nayaina sammā kittaitvā sammatā| tatraivaṃ saṅkhaipatau nimittupagatā vaiditabbā – suddhapaṃsusuddhapāsāṇaubhayamissakavasaina hi tividhaupi pabbatau hatthippamāṇatau paṭṭhāya uddhaṃ nimittupagau, tatau aumakatarau na vaṭṭati| pāsāṇanimittai ayauguḷampi pāsāṇasaṅkhamaiva gacchati, tasmā yau kauci pāsāṇau uk‍kaṃsavasaina hatthippamāṇatau aumakataraṃ ādiṃ katvā haiṭṭhimaparicchaidaina  dvattiṃsapalaguḷapiṇḍaparimāṇau nimittupagau, na tatau khuddakatarau| piṭṭhipāsāṇau pana atimahantaupi vaṭṭati| vananimittai antausāraihi vā antausāramissakaihi vā rukkhaihi catupañ‍carukkhamattampi vanaṃ nimittupagaṃ, tatau ūnakataraṃ na vaṭṭati| rukkhau jīvantauyaiva antausārau bhūmiyaṃ patiṭṭhitau, antamasau ubbaidhatau aṭṭhaṅgulau, pariṇāhatau sūcidaṇḍakappamāṇaupi nimittupagau, tatau aumakatarau na vaṭṭati| maggau jaṅghamaggau vā hautu sakaṭamaggau vā, yau vinivijjhitvā dvai tīṇi gāmakhaittāni gacchati, tādisau jaṅghasatthasakaṭasatthaihi valañ‍jiyamānauyaiva nimittupagau, avalañ‍jitau  na vaṭṭati| vammikau pana haiṭṭhimaparicchaidaina taṃdivasaṃjātau aṭṭhaṅgulubbaidhau gauvisāṇamattaupi vammikau nimittupagau, tatau aumakatarau na vaṭṭati| yaṃ pana abaddhasīmālakkhaṇai nadiṃ vakkhāma, sā nimittupagā, añ‍ñā na vaṭṭati| udakaṃ yaṃ asandamānaṃ āvāṭapaukkharaṇītaḷākajātassaralauṇisamuddādīsu ṭhitaṃ, taṃ ādiṃ katvā antamasau taṅkhaṇaiyaiva pathaviyaṃ khaṇitai āvāṭakai ghaṭaihi āharitvā pūritampi yāva kammavācāpariyausānā saṇṭhamānakaṃ nimittupagaṃ, itaraṃ sandamānaṃ vā vuttaparicchaidakālaṃ atiṭṭhantaṃ vā bhājanagataṃ vā na vaṭṭatīti|

parisāsampattiyā yuttā nāma sabbantimaina paricchaidaina catūhi bhikkhūhi san‍nipatitvā yāvatikā tasmiṃ gāmakhaittai baddhasīmaṃ vā nadisamuddajātassarai vā anauk‍kamitvā ṭhitā bhikkhū, tai sabbai hatthapāsai vā katvā chandaṃ vā āharitvā sammatā|

kammavācāsampattiyā  yuttā nāma ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, yāvatā samantā nimittā kittitā’’tiādinā (mahāva॰ 139) nayaina vuttāya parisuddhāya ñattidutiyakammavācāya sammatā| aivaṃ aikādasa vipattisīmāyau atik‍kamitvā tividhasampattiyuttā nimittaina nimittaṃ sambandhitvā sammatā sīmā ‘‘baddhasīmā’’ti vaiditabbā| khaṇḍasīmā samānasaṃvāsasīmā avippavāsasīmāti tassāyaiva pabhaidau|

abaddhasīmā pana gāmasīmā, sattabbhantarasīmā, udakukkhaipasīmāti tividhā| tattha yāvatā aikaṃ gāmakkhaittaṃ, ayaṃ gāmasīmā nāma| agāmakai arañ‍ñai samantā sattabbhantarā sattabbhantarasīmā nāma| tattha agāmakaṃ nāma arañ‍ñaṃ viñjhāṭaviādīsu vā samuddamajjhai vā macchabandhānaṃ agamanapathaisu dīpakaisu labbhati| samantā sattabbhantarāti majjhai ṭhitānaṃ sabbadisāsu  sattabbhantarā vinibbaidhaina cuddasa haunti| tattha aikaṃ abbhantaraṃ aṭṭhavīsatihatthappamāṇaṃ hauti, ayañ‍ca sīmā parisāvasaina vaḍḍhati, tasmā samantā parisāpariyantatau paṭṭhāya abbhantaraparicchaidau kātabbau| sacai pana dvai saṅghā visuṃ upausathaṃ karaunti, dvin‍naṃ sattabbhantarānaṃ antarai añ‍ñamaikaṃ sattabbhantaraṃ upacāratthāya ṭhapaitabbaṃ| yā panaisā ‘‘sabbā, bhikkhavai, nadī asīmā’’tiādinā (mahāva॰ 147) nayaina nadiādīnaṃ sīmabhāvaṃ paṭikkhipitvā puna ‘‘nadiyā vā, bhikkhavai, samuddai vā jātassarai vā yaṃ majjhimassa purisassa samantā udakukkhaipā, ayaṃ tattha samānasaṃvāsā aikūpausathā’’ti vuttā ayaṃ udakukkhaipasīmā nāma| tattha yassā dhammikānaṃ rājūnaṃ kālai anvaḍḍhamāsaṃ anudasāhaṃ anupañ‍cāhaṃ  anatik‍kamitvā daivai vassantai valāhakaisu vigatamattaisu sautaṃ pacchij‍jati, ayaṃ nadisaṅkhyaṃ na gacchati| yassā pana īdisai suvuṭṭhikālai vassānassa catumāsai sautaṃ na pacchij‍jati, yattha titthaina vā atitthaina vā sikkhākaraṇīyai āgatalakkhaṇaina timaṇḍalaṃ paṭicchādaitvā antaravāsakaṃ anukkhipitvā uttarantiyā bhikkhuniyā aikadvaṅgulamattampi antaravāsakau taimiyati, ayaṃ samuddaṃ vā pavisatu taḷākaṃ vā, pabhavatau paṭṭhāya nadī nāma| samuddau pākaṭauyaiva| yau pana kainaci khaṇitvā akatau sayaṃjātau saubbhau samantatau āgataina udakaina  pūritau tiṭṭhati, yattha nadiyaṃ vuttappakārai vassakālai udakaṃ santiṭṭhati, ayaṃ jātassarau nāma| yaupi nadiṃ vā samuddaṃ vā bhinditvā nikkhantaudakaina khatau saubbhau aitaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ pāpuṇāti, ayampi jātassarauyaiva|

yaṃ majjhimassa purisassa samantā udakukkhaipāti yaṃ ṭhānaṃ thāmamajjhimassa purisassa samantatau udakukkhaipaina paricchin‍naṃ, tattha yathā akkhadhuttā dāruguḷaṃ khipanti, aivaṃ udakaṃ vā vālukaṃ vā hatthaina gahaitvā majjhimaina purisaina sabbathāmaina khipitabbaṃ, tattha yattha aivaṃ khittaṃ udakaṃ vā vālukaṃ vā patati, ayaṃ udakukkhaipau nāma|

ayaṃ tattha samānasaṃvāsā aikūpausathāti ayaṃ taisu nadiādīsu udakukkhaipaparicchin‍nā sīmā samānasaṃvāsā caiva aikūpausathā ca, ayaṃ pana aitaisaṃ nadiādīnaṃ antauyaiva labbhati, na bahi| tasmā nadiyā vā jātassarai vā yattakaṃ padaisaṃ pakativassakālai catūsu māsaisu udakaṃ auttharati, samuddai yasmiṃ padaisai pakativīciyau auttharitvā saṇṭhahanti, tatau paṭṭhāya kappiyabhūmi, tattha ṭhatvā upausathādikammaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭati| dubbuṭṭhikālai vā gimhai vā nadijātassaraisu sukkhaisupi sā aiva kappiyabhūmi, sacai pana sukkhai jātassarai vāpiṃ vā khaṇanti, vappaṃ vā karaunti, taṃ ṭhānaṃ gāmakkhaittaṃ hauti| yā panaisā ‘‘kappiyabhūmī’’ti vuttā, tatau bahi udakukkhaipasīmā na gacchati, antauyaiva gacchati, tasmā taisaṃ antau parisāpariyantatau paṭṭhāya samantā udakukkhaipaparicchaidau kātabbau| sacai pana dvai saṅghā visuṃ visuṃ upausathādikammaṃ karaunti, dvin‍naṃ udakukkhaipānaṃ antarai añ‍ñau aikau udakukkhaipau upacāratthāya ṭhapaitabbau| ayañhi sattabbhantarasīmā ca udakukkhaipasīmā ca bhikkhūnaṃ ṭhitaukāsatau paṭṭhāya labbhati| paricchaidabbhantarai hatthapāsaṃ vijahitvā ṭhitaupi paricchaidatau bahi añ‍ñaṃ tattakaṃyaiva paricchaidaṃ anatik‍kamitvā ṭhitaupi kammaṃ kaupaiti, idaṃ sabbaaṭṭhakathāsu (mahāva॰ aṭṭha॰ 147) san‍niṭṭhānaṃ| aivaṃ abaddhasīmā vaiditabbā| iti imaṃ baddhasīmābaddhasīmāvasaina duvidhaṃ sīmaṃ sandhāyaitaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘tai ca khau hatthapāsaṃ avijahitvā aikasīmāyaṃ  ṭhitā’’ti| taisu hi catūsu bhikkhūsu aikasīmāyaṃ hatthapāsaṃ avijahitvā ṭhitaisvaivaitaṃ  saṅghassa upausathakammaṃ pattakal‍laṃ nāma hauti, na itarathā| yathāha ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitu’’nti (mahāva॰ 168)|

sabhāgāpattiyau ca na vij‍jantīti aittha yaṃ sabbau saṅghau vikālabhaujanādinā sabhāgavatthunā lahukāpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, aivarūpā vatthusabhāgā ‘‘sabhāgā’’ti vuc‍cati, vikālabhaujanapac‍cayā āpan‍naṃ pana āpattisabhāgaṃ anatirittabhaujanapac‍cayā āpan‍nassa santikai daisaituṃ vaṭṭati| sabhāgāpattiyā pana sati taihi bhikkhūhi aikau bhikkhu sāmantā āvāsā saj‍jukaṃ pāhaitabbau ‘‘gacchāvusau, taṃ āpattiṃ paṭikaritvā āgaccha, mayaṃ tai santikai āpattiṃ paṭikarissāmā’’ti, aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai labhaitha, byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ sabbau saṅghau sabhāgaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau, yadā añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ suddhaṃ anāpattikaṃ passissati, tadā tassa santikai taṃ āpattiṃ paṭikarissatī’’ti (mahāva॰ 171) vatvā upausathau kātabbau| sacai pana vaimatikau hauti, ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ sabbau saṅghau sabhāgāya āpattiyā vaimatikau, yadā nibbaimatikau bhavissati, tadā taṃ āpattiṃ paṭikarissatī’’ti vatvā upausathau kātabbau| sacai panaittha kauci taṃ sabhāgaṃ āpattiṃ daisaituṃ vaṭṭatīti mañ‍ñamānau aikassa santikai daisaiti, daisitā sudaisitāva| añ‍ñaṃ pana daisanāpac‍cayā daisakau, paṭiggahaṇapac‍cayā paṭiggāhakau cāti ubhaupi duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍janti, taṃ nānāvatthukaṃ hauti, tasmā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ daisaitabbaṃ| aittāvatā tai dvai nirāpattikā haunti, taisaṃ santikai saisaihi sabhāgāpattiyau daisaitabbā vā āraucaitabbā vā| sacai tai aivaṃ akatvā upausathaṃ karaunti, ‘‘pārisuddhiṃ āyasmantau āraucaithā’’tiādinā (mahāva॰ 134) nayaina sāpattikassa upausathakaraṇai pañ‍ñattaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍janti| sacai sabbau saṅghau sabhāgāpattiyā sati vuttavidhiṃ akatvā upausathaṃ karauti, vuttanayainaiva sabbau saṅghau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, tasmā sabhāgāpattiyā sati saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ  nāma na hauti, taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sabhāgāpattiyau ca na vij‍jantī’’ti| aitāsu hi sabhāgāpattīsu avij‍jamānāsu visabhāgāpattīsu vij‍jamānāsupi pattakal‍laṃ hautiyaiva|

vaj‍janīyā ca puggalā tasmiṃ na hauntīti ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sagahaṭṭhāya parisāya pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ, yau uddisaiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti  (mahāva॰ 154) vacanatau gahaṭṭhau, ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā nisin‍naparisāya pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabba’’ntiādinā (mahāva॰ 183) nayaina vuttā bhikkhunī, sikkhamānā, sāmaṇairau, sāmaṇairī, sikkhāpac‍cakkhātakau, antimavatthuajjhāpan‍nakau, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakau, paṇḍakau, thaiyyasaṃvāsakau, titthiyapak‍kantakau, tiracchānagatau, mātughātakau, pitughātakau arahantaghātakau, bhikkhunidūsakau, saṅghabhaidakau, lauhituppādakau, ubhataubyañ‍janakauti imai vīsati cāti aikavīsati puggalā vaj‍janīyā nāma, tai hatthapāsatau bahikaraṇavasaina vaj‍jaitabbā| aitaisu hi tividhai ukkhittakai sati upausathaṃ karauntau saṅghau pācittiyaṃ āpaj‍jati, saisaisu duk‍kaṭaṃ| aittha ca tiracchānagatauti yassa upasampadā paṭikkhittā, titthiyā gahaṭṭhainaiva saṅgahitā| aitaipi hi vaj‍janīyā nāma| aivaṃ pattakal‍laṃ imaihi catūhi aṅgaihi saṅgahitanti vaiditabbaṃ|

saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyyātiiminā yai tai aparaipi tayau upausathā saṅghai upausathau, gaṇai upausathau, puggalai upausathauti, aivaṃ kārakavasaina tayau upausathā vuttā, taisu itarai dvai paṭikkhipitvā saṅghai upausathamaiva dīpaiti| pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyyātiiminā yai tai aparaipi tayau upausathā suttuddaisau, pārisuddhiupausathau, adhiṭṭhānaupausathauti, aivaṃ kattabbākāravasaina tayau upausathā vuttā, taisu itarai dvai paṭikkhipitvā suttuddaisamaiva dīpaiti| suttuddaisau nāma pātimaukkhuddaisau vuc‍cati, sau duvidhau auvādapātimaukkhuddaisau ca āṇāpātimaukkhuddaisau ca| tattha

‘‘khantī  paramaṃ tapau titikkhā…pai॰…||

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ…pai॰…||

‘‘anūpavādau anūpaghātau’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ 2.90; dha॰ pa॰ 184, 183, 185)

ādinā nayaina vuttā tissau gāthāyau auvādapātimaukkhaṃ nāma, taṃ buddhā aiva uddisanti, na sāvakā| ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau’’tiādinā (mahāva॰ 134) nayaina vuttaṃ āṇāpātimaukkhaṃ nāma, taṃ sāvakā aiva uddisanti, na buddhā| idamaiva ca imasmiṃ atthai ‘‘pātimaukkha’’nti adhippaitaṃ|

yai  pana itarai dvai upausathā, taisu pārisuddhiupausathau tāva añ‍ñaisañ‍ca santikai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñañ‍ca āraucanavasaina duvidhau| tattha yvāyaṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ santikai karīyati, saupi pavāritānañ‍ca appavāritānañ‍ca santikai karaṇavasaina duvidhau| tattha mahāpavāraṇāya pavāritānaṃ santikai pacchimikāya upagataina vā anupagataina vā chin‍navassaina vā cātumāsiniyaṃ pana pavāritānaṃ santikai purimikāya upagataina vā anupagataina vā chin‍navassaina vā kāyasāmaggiṃ datvā ‘‘parisuddhau ahaṃ, bhantai, ‘parisuddhau’ti maṃ dhāraithā’’ti tikkhattuṃ vatvā kātabbau, ṭhapaitvā ca pana pavāraṇādivasaṃ añ‍ñasmiṃ kālai āvāsikaihi uddiṭṭhamattai pātimaukkhai avuṭṭhitāya vā aikac‍cāya vuṭṭhitāya vā sabbāya vā vuṭṭhitāya parisāya yai añ‍ñai samasamā vā thaukatarā vā āgacchanti, taihi taisaṃ santikai vuttanayainaiva pārisuddhi āraucaitabbā| yau panāyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ āraucanavasaina karīyati, sau ñattiṃ ṭhapaitvā ca aṭṭhapaitvā ca karaṇavasaina duvidhau| tattha yasmiṃ āvāsai tayau bhikkhū viharanti, taisu upausathadivasai san‍nipatitaisu aikaina bhikkhunā ‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā aj‍jupausathau cātuddasau’’ti vā ‘‘pan‍narasau’’ti vā vatvā ‘‘yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ mayaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ karaiyyāmā’’ti ñattiyā ṭhapitāya thairaina bhikkhunā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā ‘‘parisuddhau ahaṃ, āvusau, ‘parisuddhau’ti maṃ dhāraithā’’ti (mahāva॰ 168) tikkhattuṃ vattabbaṃ| itaraihi ‘‘bhantai’’ti vatvā aivamaiva vattabbaṃ| aivaṃ ñattiṃ ṭhapaitvā kātabbau| yattha pana dvai bhikkhū viharanti, tatra  ñattiṃ aṭṭhapaitvā vuttanayainaiva pārisuddhi āraucaitabbāti ayaṃ pārisuddhiupausathau|

sacai pana aikauva bhikkhu hauti, sabbaṃ pubbakaraṇīyaṃ katvā añ‍ñaisaṃ anāgamanaṃ ñatvā ‘‘aj‍ja mai upausathau cātuddasau’’ti vā ‘‘pan‍narasau’’ti vā vatvā ‘‘adhiṭṭhāmī’’ti vattabbaṃ| ayaṃ adhiṭṭhānupausathauti aivaṃ kattabbākāravasaina tayau upausathāti vaiditabbā| aittāvatā nava upausathā dīpitā haunti| taisu divasavasaina pan‍narasikau, kārakavasaina saṅghupausathau, kattabbākāravasaina suttuddaisauti aivaṃ tilakkhaṇasampan‍nau upausathau idha niddiṭṭhauti vaiditabbau| tasmiṃ pavattamānai upausathaṃ akatvā tadahupausathai añ‍ñaṃ abhikkhukaṃ nānāsaṃvāsakaihi vā sabhikkhukaṃ āvāsaṃ vā anāvāsaṃ vā vāsatthāya añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti|

kiṃ saṅghassa pubbakic‍canti ‘‘saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyyā’’ti aivaṃ  upausathakaraṇasambandhainaiva vuttassa  saṅghassa upausathai kattabbai yaṃ taṃ ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upausathāgāraṃ sammaj‍jitu’’ntiādinā (mahāva॰ 159) nayaina pāḷiyaṃ āgataṃ, aṭṭhakathāsu ca –

‘‘sammaj‍janī padīpau ca, udakaṃ āsanaina ca|

upausathassa aitāni, ‘pubbakaraṇa’nti vuc‍cati||

‘‘chandapārisuddhiutukkhānaṃ, bhikkhugaṇanā ca auvādau|

upausathassa aitāni, ‘pubbakic‍ca’nti vuc‍catī’’ti|| (mahāva॰ aṭṭha॰ 168)|

aivaṃ dvīhi nāmaihi navavidhaṃ pubbakic‍caṃ dassitaṃ, kiṃ taṃ katanti pucchati| na hi taṃ akatvā upausathaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭati, tasmā thairaina āṇattaina agilānaina bhikkhunā upausathāgāraṃ sammaj‍jitabbaṃ, pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ, āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitabbaṃ, padīpau kātabbau, akarauntau duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jati, thairaināpi patirūpaṃ ñatvā āṇāpaitabbaṃ|

chandapārisuddhīti aittha upausathakaraṇatthaṃ san‍nipatitai saṅghai bahi upausathaṃ katvā āgataina san‍nipatitaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā kāyasāmaggiṃ adaintaina  chandau dātabbau| yaupi gilānau vā hauti kic‍cappasutau vā, taināpi pārisuddhiṃ daintaina chandaupi dātabbau| kathaṃ dātabbau? aikassa bhikkhunau santikai ‘‘chandaṃ dammi, chandaṃ mai hara, chandaṃ mai āraucaihī’’ti (mahāva॰ 165) ayaṃ atthau kāyaina vā vācāya vā ubhayaina vā viñ‍ñāpaitabbau, aivaṃ din‍nau hauti chandau| akatūpausathaina pana gilānaina vā kic‍cappasutaina vā pārisuddhi dātabbā| kathaṃ dātabbā? aikassa bhikkhunau santikai ‘‘pārisuddhiṃ dammi, pārisuddhiṃ mai hara, pārisuddhiṃ mai āraucaihī’’ti (mahāva॰ 164) ayaṃ atthau kāyaina vā vācāya vā ubhayaina vā viñ‍ñāpaitabbau, aivaṃ din‍nā hauti pārisuddhi, taṃ pana daintaina chandaupi dātabbau| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tadahupausathai pārisuddhiṃ daintaina chandampi dātuṃ, santi saṅghassa karaṇīya’’nti (mahāva॰ 165)| tattha pārisuddhidānaṃ saṅghassapi attanaupi upausathakaraṇaṃ sampādaiti, na avasaisaṃ saṅghakic‍caṃ| chandadānaṃ saṅghassaiva upausathakaraṇañ‍ca saisakic‍cañ‍ca sampādaiti, attanau pana upausathau akatauyaiva hauti| tasmā pārisuddhiṃ daintaina chandaupi dātabbau| pubbai vuttaṃ pana suddhikacchandaṃ vā imaṃ vā chandapārisuddhiṃ aikaina bahūnampi āharituṃ vaṭṭati| sacai pana sau antarāmaggai añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā yaisaṃ taina chandau vā pārisuddhi  vā gahitā, taisañ‍ca attanau ca chandapārisuddhiṃ daiti, tassaiva sā āgacchati, itarā pana biḷālasaṅkhalikā chandapārisuddhi nāma hauti, sā nāgacchati, tasmā sayamaiva san‍nipatitaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā āraucaitabbaṃ| sacai pana sañ‍cic‍ca nāraucaiti, duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jati| chandapārisuddhi pana tasmiṃ hatthapāsaṃ upagatamattaiyaiva āgatā hauti|

utukkhānanti  ‘‘haimantādīnaṃ utūnaṃ aittakaṃ atik‍kantaṃ, aittakaṃ avasiṭṭha’’nti aivaṃ utūnaṃ ācikkhanaṃ| bhikkhugaṇanāti ‘‘aittakā bhikkhū upausathaggai san‍nipatitā’’ti bhikkhūnaṃ gaṇanā| idampi hi ubhayaṃ katvāva upausathau kātabbau| auvādauti bhikkhunauvādau| na hi bhikkhunīhi yācitaṃ auvādaṃ anāraucaitvā upausathaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭati| bhikkhuniyau hi ‘‘svai upausathau’’ti āgantvā ‘‘ayaṃ upausathau cātuddasau pan‍narasau’’ti pucchitvā puna upausathadivasai āgantvā ‘‘bhikkhunisaṅghau, ayya, bhikkhusaṅghassa  pādai vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācati, labhatu kira, ayya, bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti (cūḷava॰ 413) aivaṃ auvādaṃ yācanti| taṃ ṭhapaitvā bālagilānagamiyai añ‍ñau sacaipi ārañ‍ñikau hauti, apaṭiggahaituṃ na labhati, tasmā yaina sau paṭiggahitau, taina bhikkhunā upausathaggai pātimaukkhuddaisakau bhikkhu aivaṃ vattabbau ‘‘bhikkhunisaṅghau, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghassa pādai vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācati, labhatu kira, bhantai, bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti| pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vattabbaṃ ‘‘atthi kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau’’ti| sacai hauti kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, tatau taina sau vattabbau ‘‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, taṃ bhikkhunisaṅghau upasaṅkamatū’’ti (cūḷava॰ 413)| sacai natthi, tatau taina pucchitabbaṃ ‘‘kau āyasmā ussahati bhikkhuniyau auvaditu’’nti| sacai kauci ussahati, saupi ca aṭṭhahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau, taṃ tatthaiva samman‍nitvā auvādapaṭiggāhakau vattabbau ‘‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, taṃ bhikkhunisaṅghau upasaṅkamatū’’ti (cuḷava॰ 413)| sacai pana kauci na ussahati, pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vattabbaṃ ‘‘natthi kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, pāsādikaina bhikkhunisaṅghau sampādaitū’’ti| aittāvatā hi sikkhattayasaṅgahitaṃ sakalaṃ sāsanaṃ āraucitaṃ hauti| taina bhikkhunā ‘‘sādhū’’ti sampaṭicchitvā pāṭipadadivasai bhikkhunīnaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

bhikkhunisaṅghaināpi tā bhikkhuniyau paisaitabbā, gacchatha, ayyā, pucchatha ‘‘kiṃ, ayya, labhati bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti, tāhi ‘‘sādhu, ayyai’’ti sampaṭicchitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vattabbaṃ ‘‘kiṃ, ayya, labhati bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti| taina vattabbaṃ  ‘‘natthi kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, pāsādikaina bhikkhunisaṅghau sampādaitū’’ti, tāhi ‘‘sādhu ayyā’’ti sampaṭicchitabbaṃ| idañ‍ca aikatau āgatānaṃ dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ vā vasaina vuttaṃ| tāsu pana aikāya bhikkhuniyā vattabbañ‍caiva sampaṭicchitabbañ‍ca, itarā tassā sahāyikā| sacai pana bhikkhusaṅghau vā bhikkhunisaṅghau vā na pūrati, ubhayataupi vā gaṇamattamaiva puggalamattaṃ vā hauti|

tatrāyaṃ  vacanak‍kamau – ‘‘bhikkhuniyau, ayya, bhikkhusaṅghassa pādaivandanti, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācanti, labhantu kira, ayya, bhikkhuniyau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti, ‘‘ahaṃ, ayya, bhikkhusaṅghassa pādai vandāmi, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācāmi, labhāmahaṃ, ayya, auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti, ‘‘bhikkhunisaṅghau, ayyā, ayyānaṃ pādai vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācati, labhatu kira, ayyā, bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti| ‘‘bhikkhuniyau, ayyā, ayyānaṃ pādai vandanti, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācanti, labhantu kira, ayyā, bhikkhuniyau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti, ‘‘ahaṃ, ayyā, ayyānaṃ pādai vandāmi, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācāmi, labhāmahaṃ, ayyā, auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti, ‘‘bhikkhunisaṅghau, ayya, ayyassa pādai vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācati, labhatu kira, ayya, bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti| ‘‘bhikkhuniyau, ayya, ayyassa pādai vandanti, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācanti, labhantu kira, ayya, bhikkhuniyau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti, ‘‘ahaṃ, ayya, ayyassa pādai vandāmi, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācāmi, labhāmahaṃ, ayya, auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti| taināpi bhikkhunā upausathakālai aivaṃ vattabbaṃ ‘‘bhikkhuniyau, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghassa pādai vandanti, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācanti, labhantu kira, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti, ‘‘bhikkhunī, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghassa pādai vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācati, labhatu kira, bhantai, bhikkhunī auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti| ‘‘bhikkhunisaṅghau, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau, bhantai, bhikkhunī bhantai āyasmantānaṃ pādai vandati, vandanti, vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācati, yācanti, yācati, labhatu kira, bhantai, bhikkhunisaṅghau, labhantu kira, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau, labhatu kira, bhantai, bhikkhunī auvādūpasaṅkamana’’nti| upausathaggaipi pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vā ñattiṭṭhapakaina vā itaraina vā bhikkhunā sacai sammatau bhikkhu atthi, purimanayainaiva ‘‘taṃ bhikkhunisaṅghau, taṃ bhikkhuniyau, taṃ bhikkhunī upasaṅkamatu, upasaṅkamantu, upasaṅkamatū’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai natthi, ‘‘pāsādikaina bhikkhunisaṅghau, bhikkhuniyau, bhikkhunī sampādaitu, sampādaintu, sampādaitū’’ti vattabbaṃ| auvādappaṭiggāhakaina pāṭipadai taṃ pac‍cāharitvā tathaiva vattabbaṃ, ayamaittha saṅkhaipavinicchayau| aivaṃ bhikkhunīhi yācitaṃ auvādaṃ āraucaitvāva upausathau kātabbau| taina vuttaṃ –

‘‘chandapārisuddhiutukkhānaṃ, bhikkhugaṇanā ca auvādau|

upausathassa aitāni, ‘pubbakic‍ca’nti vuc‍catī’’ti||

pārisuddhiṃ āyasmantau āraucaithāti attanau parisuddhabhāvaṃ āraucaitha, ‘‘pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmī’’tiidaṃ pārisuddhiāraucanassa kāraṇavacanaṃ| ‘‘na ca, bhikkhavai, sāpattikaina pātimaukkhaṃ sautabbaṃ, yau suṇaiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti (cūḷava॰ 386) hi vacanatau aparisuddhaihi pātimaukkhaṃ sautuṃ na vaṭṭati| taina vuttaṃ – pārisuddhiṃ āyasmantau āraucaitha, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmīti| aittha siyā ‘‘saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyya, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyyā’’ti (mahāva॰ 134) vuttattā idhāpi ‘‘pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissatī’’ti vattabbaṃ, aivañhi sati pubbaināparaṃ sandhiyatīti| vuc‍catai, vacanamattamaivaitaṃ na sandhiyati, lakkhaṇatau pana samaiti, saṅghassa sāmaggiyā, gaṇassa sāmaggiyā, puggalassa uddaisā saṅghassa uddiṭṭhaṃ hauti pātimaukkhanti idañhaittha lakkhaṇaṃ, tasmā ‘‘pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmī’’ti idamaivaittha vattabbaṃ|

taṃ sabbaiva santā sādhukaṃ suṇauma manasi karaumāti nti pātimaukkhaṃ| sabbaiva santāti yāvatikā tassā parisāya thairā ca navā ca majjhimā ca| sādhukaṃ suṇaumāti aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi karitvā sautadvāravasaina sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharāma| manasi karaumāti aikaggacittā hutvā cittai ṭhapaiyyāma| aittha ca kiñ‍cāpi ‘‘pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmī’’ti vuttattā ‘‘suṇautha manasi karauthā’’ti vattuṃ yuttaṃ viya dissati, ‘‘saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyyā’’tiiminā pana na samaiti| samaggassa hi saṅghassaitaṃ upausathakaraṇaṃ, pātimaukkhuddaisakau ca saṅghapariyāpan‍nauva, ic‍cassa saṅghapariyāpan‍nattā ‘‘suṇauma manasi karaumā’’ti idamaiva vattuṃ yuttaṃ|

idāni yaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘pārisuddhiṃ āyasmantau āraucaithā’’ti, tattha yathā pārisuddhiāraucanaṃ hauti, taṃ dassaituṃ yassa siyā āpatti, sau āvikaraiyyāti āha| tattha yassa siyāti yassa chan‍naṃ ākārānaṃ añ‍ñataraina āpan‍nāpatti bhavaiyya| āpattiñhi  āpaj‍jantau alaj‍jitā, añ‍ñāṇatā, kuk‍kuc‍cappakatatā, akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñitā, kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñitā, satisammausāti imaihi chahākāraihi (pari॰ 295) āpaj‍jati|

kathaṃ alaj‍jitāya āpaj‍jati? akappiyabhāvaṃ jānantauyaiva madditvā vītik‍kamaṃ karauti|

vuttampi  caitaṃ –

‘‘sañ‍cic‍ca āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, āpattiṃ parigūhati|

agatigamanañ‍ca gacchati, aidisau vuc‍cati alaj‍jipuggalau’’ti|| (pari॰ 359)|

kathaṃ añ‍ñāṇatāya āpaj‍jati? añ‍ñāṇapuggalau hi mandau maumūhau kattabbākattabbaṃ ajānantau akattabbaṃ karauti, kattabbaṃ virādhaiti, aivaṃ añ‍ñāṇatāya āpaj‍jati|

kathaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cappakatatāya āpaj‍jati? kappiyākappiyaṃ  nissāya kuk‍kuc‍cai uppan‍nai vinayadharaṃ pucchitvā kappiyaṃ cai, kattabbaṃ siyā, akappiyaṃ cai, na kattabbaṃ, ayaṃ pana ‘‘vaṭṭatī’’ti madditvā vītik‍kamatiyaiva, aivaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cappakatatāya āpaj‍jati|

kathaṃ akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñitāya āpaj‍jati? acchamaṃsaṃ ‘‘sūkaramaṃsa’’nti khādati, vikālai kālasañ‍ñāya bhuñ‍jati, aivaṃ akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñitāya āpaj‍jati|

kathaṃ kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñitāya āpaj‍jati? sūkaramaṃsaṃ ‘‘acchamaṃsa’’nti khādati, kālai vikālasañ‍ñāya bhuñ‍jati, aivaṃ kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñitāya āpaj‍jati|

kathaṃ satisammausā āpaj‍jati? sahasaiyyacīvaravippavāsādīni satisammausā āpaj‍jati, iti imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ ākārānaṃ añ‍ñataraina ākāraina āpan‍nā yassa siyā sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ añ‍ñatarā āpatti thairassa vā navassa vā majjhimassa vāti atthau|

sau āvikaraiyyāti sau taṃ āpattiṃ daisaitu vā pakāsaitu vāti vuttaṃ hauti| asantiyā āpattiyāti yassa pana aivaṃ anāpan‍nā vā āpattiṃ  āpaj‍jitvā ca pana vuṭṭhitā vā daisitā vā āraucitā vā āpatti, tassa sā āpatti asantī nāma hauti, aivaṃ asantiyā āpattiyā tuṇhī bhavitabbaṃ| tuṇhībhāvaina khau panāyasmantai ‘‘parisuddhā’’ti vaidissāmīti tuṇhībhāvaināpi hi kāraṇaina ahaṃ āyasmantai ‘‘parisuddhā’’ic‍caiva jānissāmīti| yathā khau pana pac‍caikapuṭṭhassa vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hautīti yathā aikainaikau puṭṭhau byākaraiyya, yathā aikainaikau pac‍caikapuṭṭhau ‘‘maṃ aisa pucchatī’’ti ñatvā byākaraiyyāti vuttaṃ hauti|

aivamaivaṃ  aivarūpāya parisāya yāvatatiyaṃ anusāvitaṃ hautīti aittha aikac‍cai tāva ācariyā aivaṃ vadanti ‘‘aivamaivaṃ imissāya bhikkhuparisāya yadaitaṃ ‘yassa siyā āpatti, sau āvikaraiyya, asantiyā āpattiyā tuṇhī bhavitabbaṃ, tuṇhībhāvaina khau panāyasmantai parisuddhāti vaidissāmī’ti tikkhattuṃ anusāvitaṃ, taṃ aikamaikaina ‘maṃ aisa pucchatī’ti aivaṃ jānitabbaṃ hautīti atthau’’ti| taṃ na yuj‍jati, kasmā? atthabyañ‍janabhaidatau| anussāvanañhi nāma atthatau ca byañ‍janatau ca abhin‍naṃ hauti ‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmī’’tiādīsu (mahāva॰ 72; cūḷava॰ 3) viya, ‘‘yassa siyā’’tiādivacanattayaṃ pana atthataupi byañ‍janataupi bhin‍naṃ, tainassa anussāvanattayaṃ na yuj‍jati| yadi caitaṃ yāvatatiyānussāvanaṃ siyā, nidānuddaisai aniṭṭhitaipi āpatti siyā| na ca yuttaṃ anāpattikkhaittai āpattiṃ āpaj‍jituṃ|

aparai ‘‘anusāvita’’ntipadassa anusāvaitabbanti atthaṃ vikappaitvā ‘‘yāvatatiya’’ntiidaṃ upari uddaisāvasānai ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā…pai॰… tatiyampi pucchāmī’’ti aitaṃ sandhāya vuttanti āhu| tampi na yuj‍jati, kasmā? atthayuttīnaṃ abhāvatau| idañhi padaṃ kaici ‘‘anusāvaita’’nti sajjhāyanti, kaici ‘‘anusāvaita’’nti, taṃ ubhayaṃ vāpi atītakālamaiva dīpaiti, na anāgataṃ| yadi cassa ayaṃ atthau siyā, ‘‘anusāvitaṃ haissatī’’ti vadaiyya, aivaṃ tāva atthābhāvatau na yuj‍jati| yadi caitaṃ uddaisāvasānai vacanaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ siyā, ‘‘na āvikarissāmī’’ti cittaṃ uppādaintassa nidānai samattaipi vuttamusāvādau na siyā, kasmā? ‘‘yāvatatiyaṃ anussāviyamānai’’tivacanatau (mahāva॰ 134) ‘‘yāvatatiya’’nti idaṃ vacanamaiva  niratthakaṃ siyā, kasmā? nidānuddaisai yāvatatiyānussāvanassa abhāvatauti aivaṃ yuttiabhāvatau tampi na yuj‍jati| ‘‘yāvatatiyaṃ anusāvitaṃ hautī’’ti idaṃ pana lakkhaṇavacanamattaṃ, taina imamatthaṃ dassaiti – idaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ nāma yāvatatiyaṃ anussāviyati, tasmiṃ yāvatatiyaṃ anussāviyamānai yau saramānau santiṃ āpattiṃ nāvikarauti, tassa yāvatatiyānussāvanāvasānai sampajānamusāvādau hautīti|

tadaitaṃ yathā anusāvitaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ anusāvitaṃ nāma hauti, taṃ dassaituṃ tatthāyasmantai pucchāmītiādi vuttaṃ| taṃ panaitaṃ pārājikādīnaṃ avasānai dissati, na nidānāvasānai| kiñ‍cāpi na dissati, atha khau uddaisakālai ‘‘āvikatā hissa phāsu hautī’’ti vatvā ‘‘uddiṭṭhaṃ khau āyasmantau nidānaṃ, tatthāyasmantai pucchāmī’’tiādinā nayaina vattabbamaiva| aivañhi nidānaṃ suuddiṭṭhaṃ hauti, añ‍ñathā duuddiṭṭhaṃ| imamaiva ca atthaṃ sandhāya upausathakkhandhakai vuttaṃ ‘‘yāvatatiyaṃ anusāvitaṃ  hautīti sakimpi anusāvitaṃ hauti, dutiyampi anusāvitaṃ hauti, tatiyampi anusāvitaṃ hautī’’ti (mahāva॰ 134)| ayamaittha ācariyaparamparābhatau vinicchayau|

yau pana bhikkhu…pai॰… sampajānamusāvādassa hautīti sampajānamusāvādau assa hauti, tainassa duk‍kaṭāpatti hauti, sā ca khau pana na musāvādalakkhaṇaina, ‘‘sampajānamusāvādai kiṃ hauti, duk‍kaṭaṃ hautī’’ti (mahāva॰ 135) iminā pana bhagavatau vacanaina vacīdvārai akiriyasamuṭṭhānāpatti hautīti vaiditabbā|

vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘anālapantau manujaina kainaci,

vācāgiraṃ nau ca parai bhaṇaiyya|

āpaj‍jaiyya vācasikaṃ, na kāyikaṃ,

pañhāmaisā kusalaihi cintitā’’ti|| (pari॰ 479)|

antarāyikauti  vippaṭisāravatthutāya pāmauj‍jādisambhavaṃ nivāraitvā paṭhamajjhānādīnaṃ adhigamāya antarāyaṃ karauti| tasmāti yasmā ayaṃ anāvikaraṇasaṅkhātau sampajānamusāvādau antarāyikau hauti, tasmā| saramānaināti attani santiṃ āpattiṃ jānantaina| visuddhāpaikkhaināti vuṭṭhātukāmaina visujjhitukāmaina| santī āpattīti āpaj‍jitvā avuṭṭhitā āpatti| āvikātabbāti saṅghamajjhai vā gaṇamajjhai vā aikapuggalai vā pakāsaitabbā, antamasau anantarassāpi bhikkhunau ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, itthan‍nāmaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau, itau vuṭṭhahitvā  taṃ āpattiṃ paṭikarissāmī’’ti (mahāva॰ 170) vattabbaṃ| sacaipi vaimatikau hauti, ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, itthan‍nāmāya āpattiyā vaimatikau, yadā nibbaimatikau bhavissāmi, tadā taṃ āpattiṃ paṭikarissāmī’’ti (mahāva॰ 169) vattabbaṃ| āvikatā hissa phāsu hautītiaittha āvikatāti āvikatāya, pakāsitāyāti atthau| alaj‍jitātiādīsu (pari॰ 295) viya hi idampi karaṇatthai pac‍cattavacanaṃ| hīti nipātamattaṃ| assāti aitassa bhikkhunau| phāsu hautīti paṭhamajjhānādīnaṃ adhigamāya phāsu hauti, avippaṭisāramūlakānaṃ pāmauj‍jādīnaṃ vasaina sukhappaṭipadā sampaj‍jatīti atthau|

iti kaṅkhāvitaraṇiyā pātimaukkhavaṇṇanāya

nidānavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

pārājikakaṇḍau

idāni  yadaitaṃ nidānānantaraṃ tatrimai cattārautiādi pārājikakaṇḍaṃ, tattha tatrāti tasmiṃ ‘‘pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmī’’ti aivaṃ vuttai pātimaukkhai| imaiti idāni vattabbānaṃ abhimukhīkaraṇaṃ| cattārauti gaṇanaparicchaidau| pārājikāti aivaṃnāmakā| dhammāti āpattiyau| uddaisaṃ āgacchantīti sarūpaina uddisitabbataṃ āgacchanti, na nidānai viya ‘‘yassa siyā āpattī’’ti sādhāraṇavacanamattaina|

1. paṭhamapārājikavaṇṇanā

yau panāti rassadīghādinā liṅgādibhaidaina yau kauci| bhikkhūti aihibhikkhuupasampadā, saraṇagamanūpasampadā, auvādappaṭiggahaṇūpasampadā, pañhābyākaraṇūpasampadā, aṭṭhagarudhammappaṭiggahaṇūpasampadā, dūtainūpasampadā, aṭṭhavācikūpasampadā, ñatticatutthakammūpasampadāti imāsu aṭṭhasu upasampadāsu ñatticatutthaina upasampadākammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina upasampan‍nau| tassa pana kammassa vatthuñattianussāvana sīmā parisāsampattivasaina akuppatā vaiditabbā|

tattha vatthūti upasampadāpaikkhau puggalau, sau ṭhapaitvā ūnavīsativassaṃ antimavatthuajjhāpan‍napubbaṃ, paṇḍakādayau ca aikādasa abhabbapuggalai vaiditabbau| tattha ūnavīsativassau nāma paṭisandhiggahaṇatau paṭṭhāya aparipuṇṇavīsativassau| antimavatthuajjhāpan‍napubbau nāma catun‍naṃ pārājikānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ ajjhāpan‍napubbau| paṇḍakādayau vaj‍janīyapuggalakathāyaṃ vuttā| taisu āsittapaṇḍakañ‍ca usūyapaṇḍakañ‍ca ṭhapaitvā aupak‍kamikapaṇḍakau napuṃsakapaṇḍakau paṇḍakabhāvapakkhai ṭhitau pakkhapaṇḍakau ca idha adhippaitau|

thaiyyasaṃvāsakau pana tividhau liṅgatthainakau saṃvāsatthainakau ubhayatthainakauti| tattha yau sayaṃ pabbajitvā  na bhikkhuvassāni gaṇaiti, na yathāvuḍḍhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vā sāmaṇairānaṃ  vā vandanaṃ sādiyati, na āsanaina paṭibāhati, na upausathādīsu sandissati, ayaṃ asuddhacittatāya liṅgamattassaiva thainitattā liṅgatthainakau nāma| yau pana bhikkhūhi pabbajitau sāmaṇairau samānau kāsāyāni apanaitvā taisu saussāhauva  maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitvā puna nivāsaitvā sāmaṇairabhāvaṃ paṭijānāti, ayaṃ bhikkhūhi din‍naliṅgassa aparic‍cattattā na liṅgatthainakau, na liṅgānurūpassa saṃvāsassa sāditattā nāpi saṃvāsatthainakau| antimavatthuajjhāpan‍nakaipi aisaiva nayau| yau ca khau sāmaṇairau samānau vidaisaṃ gantvā bhikkhuvassāni gaṇaiti, yathāvuḍḍhaṃ vandanaṃ sādiyati, āsanaina paṭibāhati, upausathādīsu sandissati, ayaṃ saṃvāsamattassaiva thainitattā saṃvāsatthainakau nāma| bhikkhuvassagaṇanādikau hi sabbaupi kiriyabhaidau imasmiṃ atthai ‘‘saṃvāsau’’ti vaiditabbau| sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya ‘‘na maṃ kauci jānātī’’ti puna aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaipi aisaiva nayau| yau pana sayaṃ pabbajitvā vihāraṃ gantvā yathāvuḍḍhaṃ vandanaṃ sādiyati, āsanaina paṭibāhati, bhikkhuvassāni gaṇaiti, upausathādīsu sandissati, ayaṃ liṅgassa caiva saṃvāsassa ca thainitattā ubhayatthainakau nāma| dhuranikkhaipavasaina kāsāyāni apanaitvā antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpaj‍jitvā puna tāni acchādaitvā aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaipi aisaiva nayau, ayaṃ tividhaupi thaiyyasaṃvāsakau idha adhippaitau| ṭhapaitvā pana imaṃ tividhaṃ|

‘‘rāja dubbhikkha kantāra-rauga vairī bhayaina vā|

cīvarāharaṇatthaṃ vā, liṅgaṃ ādiyatīdha yau||

‘‘saṃvāsaṃ nādhivāsaiti, yāva sau suddhamānasau|

thaiyyasaṃvāsakau nāma, tāva aisa na vuc‍catī’’ti|| (mahāva॰ aṭṭha॰ 110)|

yau pana upasampan‍nau titthiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau sayaṃ vā kusacīrādikaṃ titthiyaliṅgaṃ ādiyati, taisaṃ vā santikai pabbajati, naggau vā hutvā ājīvakānaṃ santikaṃ gantvā taisaṃ vatāni ādiyati, ayaṃ titthiyapak‍kantakau nāma| ṭhapaitvā pana manussajātikaṃ avasaisau sabbaupi tiracchānagatau nāma| yaina manussajātikā janaitti sayampi manussabhūtainaiva sañ‍cic‍ca jīvitā vauraupitā, ayaṃ mātughātakau nāma| pitughātakaipi aisaiva nayau| yaina antamasau gihiliṅgai ṭhitaupi manussajātikau khīṇāsavau sañ‍cic‍ca jīvitā vauraupitau, ayaṃ arahantaghātakau nāma| yau pana pakatattaṃ bhikkhuniṃ tiṇṇaṃ maggānaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ  maggai dūsaiti, ayaṃ bhikkhunidūsakau nāma| yau daivadattau viya sāsanaṃ uddhammaṃ ubbinayaṃ katvā catun‍naṃ kammānaṃ añ‍ñataravasaina saṅghaṃ bhindati, ayaṃ saṅghabhaidakau nāma| yau daivadattau viya duṭṭhacittaina vadhakacittaina tathāgatassa jīvamānakasarīrai khuddakamakkhikāya pivanamattampi lauhitaṃ uppādaiti, ayaṃ lauhituppādakau nāma| yassa  itthinimittuppādanakammatau ca purisanimittuppādanakammatau ca ubhatau duvidhampi byañ‍janaṃ atthi, ayaṃ ubhataubyañ‍janakau nāma| iti imai tairasa puggalā upasampadāya avatthū, imai pana ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñasmiṃ upasampadāpaikkhai sati upasampadākammaṃ vatthusampattivasaina akuppaṃ hauti|

kathaṃ ñattisampattivasaina akuppaṃ hauti? vatthusaṅghapuggalañattīnaṃ aparāmasanāni, pacchā ñattiṭṭhapanañ‍cāti imai tāva pañ‍ca ñattidausā| tattha ‘‘ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau’’ti upasampadāpaikkhassa akittanaṃ vatthuaparāmasanaṃ nāma| ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau’’tiaittha ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai’’ti vatvā ‘‘saṅghau’’ti abhaṇanaṃ saṅghaaparāmasanaṃ nāma| ‘‘itthan‍nāmassa upasampadāpaikkhau’’ti upajjhāyassa akittanaṃ puggalaaparāmasanaṃ nāma| sabbaina sabbaṃ ñattiyā anuc‍cāraṇaṃ ñattiaparāmasanaṃ nāma| paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ niṭṭhāpaitvā ‘‘aisā ñattī’’ti vatvā ‘‘khamati saṅghassā’’ti aivaṃ ñattikittanaṃ pacchā ñattiṭṭhapanaṃ nāma| iti imaihi dausaihi vimuttāya ñattiyā sampan‍naṃ ñattisampattivasaina akuppaṃ hauti|

anussāvanavasaina akuppatāyapi vatthusaṅghapuggalānaṃ aparāmasanāni, sāvanāya hāpanaṃ, akālai sāvananti imai pañ‍ca anussāvanadausā| tattha vatthādīnaṃ aparāmasanāni ñattiyaṃ vuttasadisānaiva| tīsu pana anussāvanāsu yattha katthaci aitaisaṃ aparāmasanaṃ aparāmasanamaiva| sabbaina sabbaṃ pana kammavācaṃ avatvā catukkhattuṃ ñattikittanamaiva, atha vā pana kammavācābbhantarai akkharassa vā padassa vā anuc‍cāraṇaṃ vā duruc‍cāraṇaṃ vā sāvanāya hāpanaṃ nāma| sāvanāya anaukāsai paṭhamaṃ ñattiṃ aṭṭhapaitvā anussāvanakaraṇaṃ akālai sāvanaṃ nāma| iti imaihi dausaihi vimuttāya anussāvanāya sampan‍naṃ anussāvanasampattivasaina akuppaṃ hauti|

pubbai vuttaṃ vipattisīmālakkhaṇaṃ samatik‍kantāya pana sīmāya kataṃ sīmāsampattivasaina akuppaṃ hauti| yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, taisaṃ  anāgamanaṃ, chandārahānaṃ chandassa anāharaṇaṃ, sammukhībhūtānaṃ paṭik‍kausananti imai pana tayau parisādausā, taihi vimuttāya parisāya kataṃ parisāsampattivasaina akuppaṃ hauti| kāraṇārahattā pana satthu sāsanārahattā ṭhānārahaṃ nāma hauti| iti yau iminā aivaṃ akuppaina ṭhānārahaina ñatticatutthaina upasampadākammaina  upasampan‍nau, ayaṃ idha ‘‘bhikkhū’’ti adhippaitau| paṇṇattivaj‍jaisu pana añ‍ñaipi saṅgahaṃ gacchanti|

bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nauti yā bhikkhūnaṃ adhisīlasaṅkhātā sikkhā, tañ‍ca, yattha caitai saha jīvanti, aikajīvikā sabhāgavuttinau  haunti, taṃ bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ sikkhāpadasaṅkhātaṃ sājīvañ‍ca, tattha sikkhanabhāvaina samāpan‍nauti bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamaāpan‍nau| samāpan‍nauti sikkhañ‍ca paripūraintau sājīvañ‍ca avītik‍kamantau hutvā tadubhayaṃ upagatauti atthau| sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvāti yaṃ sikkhaṃ samāpan‍nau, taṃ apaṭikkhipitvā, yañ‍ca sājīvaṃ samāpan‍nau, tasmiṃ dubbalabhāvaṃ appakāsaitvā| tattha cittakhaittakālapayaugapuggalavijānanavasaina sikkhāya pac‍cakkhānaṃ ñatvā tadabhāvaina apac‍cakkhānaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| kathaṃ? upasampan‍nabhāvatau cavitukāmatācittainaiva hi sikkhāpac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na davā vā ravā vā bhaṇantassa| aivaṃ cittavasaina sikkhāpac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na tadabhāvaina| tathā ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cakkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cakkhāmi, sikkhaṃ, vinayaṃ, pātimaukkhaṃ, uddaisaṃ, upajjhāyaṃ, ācariyaṃ, saddhivihārikaṃ, antaivāsikaṃ, samānupajjhāyakaṃ, samānācariyakaṃ, sabrahmacāriṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’’ti aivaṃ vuttānaṃ buddhādīnaṃ cuddasan‍naṃ, ‘‘gihīti maṃ dhāraihi, upāsakau, ārāmikau, sāmaṇairau, titthiyau, titthiyasāvakau, asamaṇau, ‘asakyaputtiyau’ti maṃ dhāraihī’’ti aivaṃ vuttānaṃ gihiādīnaṃ aṭṭhan‍nañ‍cāti imaisaṃ dvāvīsatiyā khaittapadānaṃ yassa kassaci savaivacanassa vasaina taisu yaṃkiñ‍ci vattukāmassa yaṃkiñ‍ci vadatau sikkhāpac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na rukkhādīnaṃ añ‍ñatarassa nāmaṃ gahaitvā pac‍cācikkhantassa| aivaṃ khaittavasaina pac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na tadabhāvaina|

tattha yadaitaṃ ‘‘pac‍cakkhāmī’’ti ca, ‘‘maṃ dhāraihī’’ti (pārā॰ 51) ca vuttaṃ vattamānakālavacanaṃ, yāni ca ‘‘alaṃ mai buddhaina, kiṃ nu mai buddhaina, na mamatthau buddhaina, sumuttāhaṃ buddhainā’’tiādinā (pārā॰ 52) nayaina ākhyātavasaina kālaṃ anāmasitvā purimaihi cuddasahi padaihi saddhiṃ yaujaitvā vuttāni ‘‘alaṃ mai’’tiādīni cattāri padāni, taisaṃyaiva ca savaivacanānaṃ vasaina pac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na ‘‘pac‍cakkhāsi’’nti vā ‘‘pac‍cakkhissa’’nti vā ‘‘maṃ dhāraisī’’ti vā ‘‘maṃ dhāraissatī’’ti vā ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’’nti (pārā॰ 45) vātiādīni atītānāgataparikappavacanāni bhaṇantassa| aivaṃ vattamāna kālavasaina caiva anāmaṭṭhakālavasaina ca pac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na tadabhāvaina|

payaugau  pana duvidhau kāyikau ca vācasikau ca| tattha ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’’tiādinā (pārā॰ 51) nayaina yāya kāyaci bhāsāya vacībhaidaṃ katvā vācasikappayaugainaiva pac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na akkharalikhanaṃ vā hatthamuddādidassanaṃ vā kāyappayaugaṃ karauntassa| aivaṃ vācasikappayaugainaiva pac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na tadabhāvaina|

puggalau pana duvidhau – yau ca pac‍cakkhāti, yassa ca pac‍cakkhāti| tattha yau pac‍cakkhāti, sau sacai ummattakakhittacittavaidanāṭṭānaṃ añ‍ñatarau na hauti| yassa pana  pac‍cakkhāti, sau sacai manussajātikau hauti, na ca ummattakādīnaṃ añ‍ñatarau, sammukhībhūtau ca sikkhāpac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti| na hi asammukhībhūtassa dūtaina vā paṇṇaina vā āraucanaṃ ruhati| aivaṃ yathāvuttapuggalavasaina pac‍cakkhānaṃ hauti, na tadabhāvaina|
